# Modular graph engine, Phase B1: the native engine on the graph executor - implementation plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or
> superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for
> tracking.

**Goal:** `Engine::processBlock` runs a `GraphHost` whose plan a native translator builds from today's
structural calls, bit-identical to the B0 parity baseline, and the routers, `BlockRunner`, `LinkGroup`,
`Project`, `OfflineRender` and `AudioRouting.hpp` are deleted.

**Architecture:** Per [docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md](modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md). Today's
cores become system nodes (`rp.sameboy.gb`, `rp.mesen.nes` / `.gba` / `.sms`) over `SystemBase`; today's
kernel stage becomes `rp.dsp.kernel`, the plan's first step; a control-side translator turns add, replace,
remove, routing, link groups and the host's output shape into graph documents, each with a system table
that rides its plan; every other command rides the GraphHost's one ring as a host command, in push order
with the plan swaps. The graph engine lands behind `RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE=1`; once parity holds in both
modes, the flip makes it the only engine and deletes the old one.

**Tech stack:** C++20 (CMake), the graph layer of Phase A (`packages/native/src/graph/`), Catch2, TypeScript
on txiki for the parity rig.

**Provenance:** every code block below was built and run before this plan was written, on the scratch
branch `proto/b1` in this worktree, one commit per concern, with GCC 13.3 on linux-x64. A code block that
creates a file is that file as the prototype commit left it; one that changes a file is the prototype's
diff for it, so apply it with the Edit tool hunk by hunk (or `git apply` it from the step's text). Every
expected count and failure line is what that run printed.

**Ground rules for whoever executes this:**
- Commit after each task (the commit commands are given). No `Co-Authored-By` or "generated with" trailer.
- Do not push, and do not open a PR.
- Never use an emdash character in code, comments, docs or commit messages; use " - ", ":" or ";".
- Don't `rm -rf build` to fix CMake; investigate. Never use bare `git stash`, and never switch branches with
  uncommitted work in the tree. A new CMake target needs a reconfigure first: `cmake -S . -B build`.
- `deps/sameboy` shows as modified after any configure. That is the tracked audio patch: expected, leave it.
- Parity baseline JSON is generated by `RP_PARITY_UPDATE=1` runs, never edited by hand, and no task but
  Task 4 records one: every other task must leave every baseline passing unchanged.
- The fixture groups need `RETROPLUG_RESOURCES_DIR=/workspaces/resources`; without it their cases skip. Pass
  it on the same command line: a shell `export` does not survive between commands.
- While a Reaper check runs it uses `build/bin`: do not rebuild the plugin until it finishes.

### Task 1: The controller-MIDI inline slot

`QueuedInvoker::stageControllerMidi` copies at most 4 bytes into the command's inline slot but stores the
full length, so `drainInto` reads past the slot for a longer message (design section 9). The RPC already
caps it at 4; the invoker now refuses what it cannot hold and says so.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/native/test/audio/EngineInvoker.test.cpp`
- Modify: `packages/native/cmake/tests.cmake` (the `retroplug-audio-test` source list)
- Modify: `packages/native/src/host/engine/EngineInvoker.{hpp,cpp}`, `packages/native/src/host/rpc/EngineRpcService.cpp`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `packages/native/test/audio/EngineInvoker.test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Guards the command ring's payload handling at its edges: a control-surface message must fit the
// command's 4-byte inline slot (drainInto reads `len` bytes out of it), and a queued per-system MIDI
// payload must be freed when teardown discards it unapplied. The leak itself only shows under
// LeakSanitizer: build this binary in build-asan/ (tools/run-sanitizer.sh's tree) and run "[invoker]".
//
// Run via `pnpm test:plugin`.

#include <vector>

#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>

#include "host/engine/Engine.hpp"
#include "host/engine/EngineInvoker.hpp"

TEST_CASE("stageControllerMidi refuses what the inline slot cannot hold", "[invoker]") {
    Engine        eng(44100.0);
    QueuedInvoker inv(eng, eng.registry());
    CHECK_FALSE(inv.stageControllerMidi({}));
    CHECK_FALSE(inv.stageControllerMidi({0xF0, 0x00, 0x20, 0x29, 0xF7}));
    CHECK(inv.stageControllerMidi({0x90, 60, 127}));
    CHECK(inv.stageControllerMidi({0xB0, 1, 2, 3}));
}
```

In `packages/native/cmake/tests.cmake`, add it to `retroplug-audio-test`'s sources after
`test/audio/SameBoyModels.test.cpp`:

```cmake
             test/audio/EngineInvoker.test.cpp         # the command ring's inline slot + teardown payload frees
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to verify it fails**

Run: `cmake -S . -B build && cmake --build build --target retroplug-audio-test -j$(nproc)`
Expected: a compile error, `CHECK_FALSE` cannot test a `void` (`stageControllerMidi` returns nothing).

- [ ] **Step 3: Make it refuse and report**

In `packages/native/src/host/engine/EngineInvoker.hpp`:

```cpp
    // Control-surface traffic (a pad press), kept out of the musical stream. Inline-only (4 bytes),
    // which a Note/CC press fits inside; a surface's sysex never comes this way. False, with nothing
    // staged, for an empty message or one longer than 4 bytes.
    bool stageControllerMidi(std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes);
```

In `packages/native/src/host/engine/EngineInvoker.cpp`, replace `stageControllerMidi`:

```cpp
bool QueuedInvoker::stageControllerMidi(std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes) {
    // Inline-only: drainInto reads `len` bytes out of the 4-byte slot, so a longer message would read past it.
    if (bytes.empty() || bytes.size() > 4) return false;
    DspCommand c;
    c.kind = DspCommand::Kind::StageControllerMidi;
    c.stageMidi.ext = nullptr;
    c.stageMidi.len = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(bytes.size());
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < bytes.size(); ++i) c.stageMidi.data[i] = bytes[i];
    commands_.tryPush(c);
    maybeFlush();
    return true;
}
```

In `packages/native/src/host/rpc/EngineRpcService.cpp`, the RPC now forwards the invoker's answer:

```cpp
bool EngineRpcService::stageControllerIn(std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes) {
    return invoker_.stageControllerMidi(std::move(bytes));  // a pad press is 3 bytes; the ring carries 4 inline
}
```

Run: `cmake --build build --target retroplug-audio-test -j$(nproc) && build/bin/retroplug-audio-test "[invoker]"`
Expected: `All tests passed (4 assertions in 1 test case)`

### Task 2: The `StageSystemMidi` teardown leak

`QueuedInvoker::freePending` discards every queued payload at teardown except a `StageSystemMidi` one,
which leaks. Only LeakSanitizer sees it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/native/test/audio/EngineInvoker.test.cpp`, `packages/native/src/host/engine/EngineInvoker.cpp`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Append to `packages/native/test/audio/EngineInvoker.test.cpp`:

```cpp

TEST_CASE("freePending frees a queued per-system MIDI payload", "[invoker]") {
    Engine        eng(44100.0);
    QueuedInvoker inv(eng, eng.registry());
    inv.setAudioThreadOwns(true);  // push-only: the command stays queued
    inv.stageSystemMidi(1, std::vector<std::uint8_t>(10, 0x42));  // past 4 bytes: an owning payload
    inv.freePending();             // teardown: discards it, and must free it
    inv.setAudioThreadOwns(false);
    SUCCEED();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it under LeakSanitizer to verify it fails**

Run: `cmake --build build-asan --target retroplug-audio-test -j$(nproc) && ASAN_OPTIONS=detect_leaks=1 build-asan/bin/retroplug-audio-test "[invoker]"`
(`build-asan/` is `tools/run-sanitizer.sh address`'s tree; run that script once if it does not exist yet.)
Expected: exit 1, `ERROR: LeakSanitizer: detected memory leaks`, `SUMMARY: AddressSanitizer: 34 byte(s) leaked in 2 allocation(s).`

- [ ] **Step 3: Free it**

In `QueuedInvoker::freePending` (`packages/native/src/host/engine/EngineInvoker.cpp`), after the `StageMidi` case:

```cpp
            case DspCommand::Kind::StageMidi:     delete cmd.stageMidi.ext; break;  // null for an inline message
            case DspCommand::Kind::StageSystemMidi: delete cmd.stageSystemMidi.ext; break;  // likewise
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run it to verify it passes**

Run the Step 2 command again. Expected: `All tests passed (5 assertions in 2 test cases)`, exit 0. Then
`cmake --build build --target retroplug-audio-test -j$(nproc) && build/bin/retroplug-audio-test "[invoker]"` passes too.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add packages/native/test/audio/EngineInvoker.test.cpp packages/native/src/host/engine/EngineInvoker.cpp
git commit -m "fix(engine): freePending frees a queued StageSystemMidi payload"
```

### Task 3: The render calls' ownership guard

`renderAudio`, `renderAudioPerSystem` and `renderAudioPerChannel` call `processBlock` on the calling
thread with no check that the audio thread is not running it (`renderBlocks` has the check). B1 relies on
the guard: those calls set the host shape, which must not change under a running audio thread.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/retroplug/test-native/render-guard.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/native/src/host/rpc/EngineRpcService.cpp`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `packages/retroplug/test-native/render-guard.test.ts`:

```ts
// The render calls drive processBlock on the CALLING thread, which only the Engine's owner may do. While
// the background audio thread owns it, they must refuse (as renderBlocks does) rather than render
// concurrently with it; once the thread has stopped they render again.
import { test, expect } from "../testing/harness";
import { createRealBackend } from "../src/realBackend";
import { createAudioDriver } from "../src/audioDriver";
import type { ConstructSpec } from "../src/backend";

const MGB: ConstructSpec = { romPath: "", platform: "gb", core: "sameboy", embeddedRom: "mgb", savPath: null, statePath: null };

test("renderAudio, renderAudioPerSystem and renderAudioPerChannel refuse while the audio thread runs", () => {
  const be = createRealBackend();
  const audio = createAudioDriver();
  expect(be.constructSystem(MGB, 1)).toBeTruthy();

  expect(audio.startAudio()).toBeTruthy();
  expect(audio.renderAudio(20).length).toBe(0);
  expect(audio.renderAudioPerSystem(20).length).toBe(0);
  expect(audio.renderAudioPerChannel(1, 20).length).toBe(0);
  expect(audio.stopAudio()).toBeTruthy();

  expect(audio.renderAudio(20).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(audio.renderAudioPerSystem(20).length).toBe(1);
  expect(audio.renderAudioPerChannel(1, 20).length).toBe(4);
  be.removeSystem(1);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to verify it fails**

Run: `cmake --build build --target retroplug-host -j$(nproc) && node packages/retroplug/scripts/run-native-tests.mjs render-guard`
Expected: `not ok 1 - renderAudio, renderAudioPerSystem and renderAudioPerChannel refuse while the audio thread runs`,
`expected 0, got 1764`.

```cpp
rfl::Bytestring EngineRpcService::renderAudio(double ms) {

```

```cpp

    std::vector<rfl::Bytestring> result;
    if (ms <= 0.0 || n == 0 || invoker_.audioThreadOwns()) return result;  // as renderAudio
```

```cpp
std::vector<rfl::Bytestring> EngineRpcService::renderAudioPerChannel(std::uint32_t id, double ms) {
    std::vector<rfl::Bytestring> result;
    if (invoker_.audioThreadOwns()) return result;  // as renderAudio
```

### Task 4: Parity cases for the two render shapes

`renderAudioPerSystem` and `renderAudioPerChannel` reach routers `renderBlocks` never does (B0 section 9),
and B1 replaces them. Recorded now, on today's engine, so B1 is held to them.

A shape segment renders through one of those calls: `ms` of audio in its own 1024-frame blocks, the
segment's MIDI staged first at frame 0, two lanes per system or per stream. Seven cases: three mixed systems
per system; a NES under ChannelSplit per system (the narrow-host defect's path); and per channel a Game Boy's
four stems and a NES in each export mode (0 by default, 1 and 3 armed by PinSplit and ChannelSplit, 2 built
from the `mesen` role and a reload).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/rig.ts`, `packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/group.ts`
- Create: `packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/render-shapes.test.ts`
- Create (generated): `packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/baseline/render-shapes.json`

- [ ] **Step 1: Teach the rig the shape segments**

`packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/rig.ts`:

```diff
diff --git a/packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/rig.ts b/packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/rig.ts
index 0b9a7ddf..01a11f2c 100644
--- a/packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/rig.ts
+++ b/packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/rig.ts
@@ -19,6 +19,12 @@ export interface Segment {
   transport?: { at: number; playing?: boolean; bpm?: number; ppq?: number }[];
   /** A store edit made after this segment renders and before the next one does. */
   after?: (rig: Rig) => void;
+  /** Render through renderAudioPerSystem (`perSystem`) or renderAudioPerChannel (`perChannel`, the system in
+   *  that project slot) instead of renderBlocks: `ms` of audio in those calls' own 1024-frame blocks, two
+   *  lanes (L, R) per system or per stream. `blocks` must be empty, and the segment's MIDI is staged first,
+   *  all of it at frame 0 through stageMidiIn, as the render library stages it. No controller traffic or
+   *  transport. These are the output shapes renderBlocks does not reach. */
+  shape?: { perSystem: true; ms: number } | { perChannel: number; ms: number };
 }
 
 /** `total` frames in blocks of `size` (the last one short). */
@@ -46,6 +52,8 @@ export class Rig {
   /** system id -> project slot, for the whole case: updated by each render with the systems live then and
    *  never pruned, so MIDI out from a system an `after` hook removed or replaced keeps its last known slot. */
   private readonly slots = new Map<number, number>();
+  /** The rate the last reset set: a shape segment turns its `ms` into frames as the render calls do. */
+  private sampleRate = 48000;
 
   /** Where this process keeps its ROM copies: under its OWN config dir (the alone-run child gets another). */
   private romDir(): string {
@@ -79,6 +87,7 @@ export class Rig {
     if (audio.systemCount() !== 0) throw new Error(`${audio.systemCount()} system(s) survived the reset`);
     this.blockBase = 0;
     this.slots.clear();
+    this.sampleRate = sampleRate;
   }
 
   /** A fresh copy of the ROM `src`, and of nothing else, in this file's temp dir, so a case never writes a
@@ -125,6 +134,7 @@ export class Rig {
 
   /** Render one segment at `outputs` lanes; returns its digest, its MIDI out, and the lanes themselves. */
   render(outputs: 2 | 4 | 6 | 8, seg: Segment): { digest: SegmentDigest; midiOut: MidiOutEntry[]; lanes: Float32Array[] } {
+    if (seg.shape) return this.renderShape(seg, seg.shape);
     const starts: number[] = [];
     let total = 0;
     const blocks: RenderBlock[] = seg.blocks.map((frames) => {
@@ -168,4 +178,31 @@ export class Rig {
     this.blockBase += blocks.length;
     return { digest: { frames: total, lanes: r.lanes.map((l) => laneDigest(l, 0, total)) }, midiOut, lanes: r.lanes };
   }
+
+  private renderShape(seg: Segment, shape: NonNullable<Segment["shape"]>): { digest: SegmentDigest; midiOut: MidiOutEntry[]; lanes: Float32Array[] } {
+    if (seg.blocks.length || seg.controller?.length || seg.transport?.length)
+      throw new Error("a shape segment renders its own blocks and takes no controller traffic or transport");
+    for (const m of [...(seg.midi ?? [])].sort((x, y) => x.at - y.at))
+      if (!this.audio.stageMidiIn(m.bytes)) throw new Error(`stageMidiIn refused ${m.bytes}`);
+    this.project.systems.view().forEach((v, i) => this.slots.set(v.id, i));
+    // The frame count the render call computes, by the same double arithmetic (EngineRpcService.cpp).
+    const total = Math.floor((shape.ms * this.sampleRate) / 1000);
+    const interleaved = "perSystem" in shape ? this.audio.renderAudioPerSystem(shape.ms) : this.audio.renderAudioPerChannel(this.id(shape.perChannel), shape.ms);
+    if (!interleaved.length) throw new Error("the render call returned nothing (no systems, or not exactly one for perChannel)");
+    const lanes: Float32Array[] = [];
+    for (const buf of interleaved) {
+      if (buf.length !== total * 2) throw new Error(`a render returned ${buf.length / 2} frames, expected ${total}`);
+      const l = new Float32Array(total);
+      const r = new Float32Array(total);
+      for (let f = 0; f < total; f++) {
+        l[f] = buf[2 * f];
+        r[f] = buf[2 * f + 1];
+      }
+      lanes.push(l, r);
+    }
+    // These calls hand back their MIDI out without block indices: it is all tagged with the segment's first.
+    const midiOut = this.audio.drainMidiOut().map((m) => ({ block: this.blockBase, slot: this.slots.get(m.system) ?? -1, frame: m.frame, data: m.data }));
+    this.blockBase += Math.ceil(total / 1024);
+    return { digest: { frames: total, lanes: lanes.map((l) => laneDigest(l, 0, total)) }, midiOut, lanes };
+  }
 }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the cases**

Create `packages/retroplug/test-native/parity/render-shapes.test.ts`:

```ts
// The two output shapes renderBlocks does not reach: renderAudioPerSystem (each system on its own pair) and
// renderAudioPerChannel (one system's streams, a pair each), which the render library's exports use. Each
// case boots through renderBlocks, then renders one shape segment with held notes staged at frame 0.
import { runGroup, type ParityCase } from "./group";
import { even, type Rig } from "./rig";
import { BOOT_MS, frames, mgb, mgbFile, ROMS } from "./scenes";
import type { AudioRouting } from "../../src/settingsEnums";

const SR = 48000;
const boot = { blocks: even(frames(SR, BOOT_MS), 512) };

/** Notes held on `channels` (no note-off: a shape segment stages everything at frame 0). Key 48 on channel 3
 *  is in range for BlipToaster's noise voice (36..67) as well as mGB's. */
const held = (channels: number[]) => channels.map((ch, i) => ({ at: 0, bytes: [0x90 | ch, ch === 3 ? 48 : 60 + 4 * i, 100] }));

function perChannel(name: string, audible: number[], setup: (rig: Rig) => void): ParityCase {
  return { name, sampleRate: SR, outputs: 2, audible, setup, segments: [boot, { blocks: [], shape: { perChannel: 0, ms: 600 }, midi: held([0, 1, 2, 3]) }] };
}

const nesUnder = (mode: AudioRouting) => (rig: Rig) => {
  rig.add(ROMS.bliptoaster);
  rig.project.setAudioRouting(mode);
};

const cases: ParityCase[] = [
  {
    // Slots 0 and 2 are both mGB: distinct gains tell them apart.
    name: "perSystem-mixed",
    sampleRate: SR,
    outputs: 2,
    audible: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
    setup: (rig) => {
      mgb(rig);
      rig.add(ROMS.bliptoaster);
      mgbFile(rig);
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) rig.project.systems.setGain(rig.id(i), -2 * i);
    },
    segments: [boot, { blocks: [], shape: { perSystem: true, ms: 600 }, midi: held([0, 1, 2, 3]) }],
  },
  {
    // One NES under ChannelSplit, rendered per system: the tap is armed but the shape asks for the mix, the
    // narrow-host defect's path (split.ts), recorded as it is.
    name: "perSystem-nes-channelSplit",
    sampleRate: SR,
    outputs: 2,
    audible: [0, 1],
    setup: nesUnder("channelSplit"),
    segments: [boot, { blocks: [], shape: { perSystem: true, ms: 600 }, midi: held([0, 1, 2, 3]) }],
  },
  // A Game Boy's four stems, a stereo pair each.
  perChannel("perChannel-mgb", [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7], (rig) => mgb(rig)),
  // A NES with no tap armed reports one stereo Mix stream.
  perChannel("perChannel-nes-mix", [0, 1], (rig) => rig.add(ROMS.bliptoaster)),
  // PinSplit arms mode 1: Pulse, TND, Expansion, mono, each on the L lane of its pair (Expansion silent on
  // the 2A03-only build).
  perChannel("perChannel-nes-pins", [0, 2], nesUnder("pinSplit")),
  // ChannelSplit arms mode 3: the five core channels, mono (DMC silent: nothing plays a sample).
  perChannel("perChannel-nes-channels", [0, 2, 4, 6], nesUnder("channelSplit")),
  // Mode 2 (pins plus the reference mix) is a build-time choice: the mesen role, then a reload builds it.
  perChannel("perChannel-nes-pinsPlusRef", [0, 2, 6], (rig) => {
    const id = rig.add(ROMS.bliptoaster);
    rig.project.systems.setRoleConfig(id, "mesen", { channelExportMode: "pinsPlusRef" });
    if (rig.project.systems.reloadSystem(id) == null) throw new Error("the reload did not build");
  }),
];

runGroup("render-shapes", cases);
```

### Task 5: `layout_for` and validate-first planning

`rp_node_type` gains `layout_for(settings)`, replacing the per-instance `layout()` (design section 6). The
planner computes every node's layout from the document first, runs every check that needs one, and only
then reconciles instances; a live instance whose layout would change is rebuilt without being asked. The
one check that needs an instance (a linked node must implement `rp.step/1`) moves after reconcile.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/native/src/graph/rp_node.h`, `node.hpp`, `NodeRegistry.cpp`, `Planner.cpp`
- Modify: `packages/native/test/graph/{Executor,NodeLayer,Registry,Planner}.test.cpp`

```diff
+     * these settings: a function of the settings alone, so the host can validate a whole document before
+     * it creates anything, and an instance's layout for its life (a settings change that would alter it
+     * is a rebuild, never a swap). Writes it NUL-terminated into out[0..capacity) when it fits and returns
+     * its length without the NUL, so a caller whose buffer is too small learns the size and asks again.
+     * Returns 0, with err filled, for settings it refuses. */
+    size_t (*layout_for)(const rp_settings* settings, char* out, size_t capacity, rp_error* err);
     /* [control] Size and alignment of one instance for these settings. */
```

```diff
+std::size_t layoutForFn(const rp_settings* s, char* out, std::size_t capacity, rp_error* err) {
+    try {
+        auto parsed = parseSettings<T>(s);
+        if (!parsed) {
+            copyError(err, parsed.error().what());
+            return 0;
+        }
+        const std::string json = graph::layoutJson(layoutFor<T>(*parsed));
+        if (out && json.size() < capacity) {
+            std::memcpy(out, json.data(), json.size());
+            out[json.size()] = '\0';
+        }
+        return json.size();
+    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
+        copyError(err, e.what());
+    } catch (...) {
+        copyError(err, "settings threw");
+    }
+    return 0;
 }
```

### Task 6: The GraphHost B1 needs

Five changes to `GraphHost` (design sections 3.4 and 5):
- **The narrowed wait.** `setGraph` waits only while a published plan still carries a settings commit, so
  plans with none queue back to back and adopt in one drain.
- **`drain()` and `run()`**, split out of `processBlock` (which keeps doing both).
- **Host commands**: an opaque fixed-size payload (`HostCommand`) on the same ring, handed at drain to the
  embedder's handler with the host data of the plan current at that point; `discardPending()` hands what is
  queued back as discarded, for teardown.
- **Per-plan host data**: `setGraph(document, hostData)`, adopted with the plan, freed with it.
- **Host services**: `HostConfig.interfaces` lets the embedder answer `rp_host.get_interface`, and
  `rp.host.audio-out` reaches the host's buffers through GraphHost's own `rp.host.audio-buffers/1`
  instead of casting `ctx`. A document with two `audio-out` nodes is refused (`audio-out-twice`).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/native/src/graph/GraphHost.{hpp,cpp}`, `PlanStorage.hpp`, `HostNodes.cpp`
- Create: `packages/native/test/graph/HostCommands.test.cpp`
- Modify: `packages/native/test/graph/SwapStress.test.cpp`, `packages/native/cmake/tests.cmake`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `packages/native/test/graph/HostCommands.test.cpp`:

```cpp
// The embedder's half of the one ring: host commands keep their push order with plan swaps and params,
// each seeing the host data of the plan current when it is applied; discardPending, at teardown, adopts
// the queued plans and hands the queued commands back as discarded; and a document gets at most one
// rp.host.audio-out.
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>

#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "GraphRig.hpp"

using namespace rp::graph;
using rp::graph::test::GraphRig;

namespace {

struct Seen {
    char cmd;
    int  data;  // the plan's host data, or -1 for none
    bool discard;
};

void record(void* ctx, const HostCommand& c, const void* hostData, bool discard) {
    static_cast<std::vector<Seen>*>(ctx)->push_back(
        {static_cast<char>(c.bytes[0]), hostData ? *static_cast<const int*>(hostData) : -1, discard});
}

SetGraphResult setWith(GraphRig& rig, const char* json, int data) {
    return rig.host->setGraph(*parseGraphDocument(json), std::make_shared<const int>(data));
}

bool push(GraphHost& h, char c) { return h.pushHostCommand(&c, 1); }

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("host commands keep push order with plan swaps, each seeing its plan's host data", "[graph][host-commands]") {
    GraphRig rig;
    std::vector<Seen> seen;
    rig.host->setHostCommandHandler(&record, &seen);
    REQUIRE(setWith(rig, R"({"nodes":[],"wires":[]})", 1).ok);
    rig.host->setAudioThreadOwns(true);
    REQUIRE(push(*rig.host, 'a'));
    REQUIRE(setWith(rig, R"({"nodes":[{"id":"s","type":"rp.test.sink"}],"wires":[]})", 2).ok);
    REQUIRE(push(*rig.host, 'b'));
    REQUIRE(setWith(rig, R"({"nodes":[],"wires":[]})", 3).ok);
    REQUIRE(push(*rig.host, 'c'));
    CHECK(seen.empty());  // push-only while the audio thread owns the host
    rig.host->drain();    // two swaps and three commands in one drain
    REQUIRE(seen.size() == 3);
    CHECK((seen[0].cmd == 'a' && seen[0].data == 1));
    CHECK((seen[1].cmd == 'b' && seen[1].data == 2));
    CHECK((seen[2].cmd == 'c' && seen[2].data == 3));
    CHECK(*static_cast<const int*>(rig.host->hostData()) == 3);
    rig.host->setAudioThreadOwns(false);
}

TEST_CASE("while quiescent a host command applies as it is pushed", "[graph][host-commands]") {
    GraphRig rig;
    std::vector<Seen> seen;
    rig.host->setHostCommandHandler(&record, &seen);
    REQUIRE(push(*rig.host, 'x'));
    REQUIRE(seen.size() == 1);
    CHECK((seen[0].cmd == 'x' && seen[0].data == -1 && !seen[0].discard));
    const std::vector<std::byte> big(kHostCommandBytes + 1);
    CHECK_FALSE(rig.host->pushHostCommand(big.data(), big.size()));
}

TEST_CASE("discardPending adopts queued plans and discards queued host commands", "[graph][host-commands]") {
    GraphRig rig;
    std::vector<Seen> seen;
    rig.host->setHostCommandHandler(&record, &seen);
    rig.host->setAudioThreadOwns(true);
    REQUIRE(setWith(rig, R"({"nodes":[{"id":"s","type":"rp.test.sink"}],"wires":[]})", 7).ok);
    REQUIRE(push(*rig.host, 'r'));
    rig.host->discardPending();  // teardown, with the audio thread stopped
    rig.host->setAudioThreadOwns(false);
    REQUIRE(seen.size() == 1);
    CHECK((seen[0].cmd == 'r' && seen[0].data == 7 && seen[0].discard));
    CHECK(rig.host->node("s") != nullptr);
}

TEST_CASE("a document gets at most one rp.host.audio-out", "[graph][host-commands]") {
    GraphRig rig;
    const auto r = rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"o1","type":"rp.host.audio-out"},{"id":"o2","type":"rp.host.audio-out"}],"wires":[]})");
    CHECK(GraphRig::codes(r) == "audio-out-twice");
}
```

```diff
     REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"a","type":"rp.test.sink"}],"wires":[]})").ok);

+    // Nothing drains these, so all three sit in the ring at once; none commits settings, so none waits.
```

```diff
 namespace rp::graph {
 
+// A host service a node asks for by id (rp_host.get_interface), offered by whoever embeds the GraphHost.
+using HostInterfaceFn = const void* (*)(void* ctx, const char* id);
+
 struct HostConfig {
     double        sampleRate = 48000.0;
     std::uint32_t maxFrames = 1024;
     Target        target = Target::Desktop;

+    // The embedder's own host services; GraphHost answers its own ids (rp.host.audio-buffers/1) first.

+    void*           interfacesCtx = nullptr;

+
+// The host's output buffers for this block, which rp.host.audio-out copies into. [audio]
+#define RP_HOST_AUDIO_BUFFERS_INTERFACE "rp.host.audio-buffers/1"
+struct rp_host_audio_buffers {
+    float* const* (*outputs)(const rp_host* host, std::uint32_t* count);
 };
 
+// An embedder's command, carried on the ring in push order with plan swaps and params, and handed back to
+// the embedder's handler at drain with the host data of the plan current at that point. Opaque here.
+constexpr std::size_t kHostCommandBytes = 48;
+struct HostCommand {
+    alignas(std::max_align_t) std::byte bytes[kHostCommandBytes];
+};
+// `discard`: the GraphHost is being destroyed with the command unapplied, so free what it owns instead.
+using HostCommandHandler = void (*)(void* ctx, const HostCommand& command, const void* hostData, bool discard);

 constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxBlockFrames = 1u << 16;
 
 // Why a rate / block size cannot be used (a positive finite rate, 1..kMaxBlockFrames frames), or nullopt.

+// while audio runs, up to a timeout if it has stalled) for the audio thread to adopt it, so the planner
+// never asks a node to prepare settings while its last commit may be running. A plan with no commit needs
+// no wait, so several can sit in the ring at once, adopted in order in one drain.

+    void setHostCommandHandler(HostCommandHandler handler, void* ctx);
     // A live param change, clamped to the param's resolved range (and rounded for int, enum and bool, as a

     bool setParam(std::string_view nodeId, std::string_view paramId, double value, std::uint32_t frame = 0);
     // Set true BEFORE the audio thread starts calling processBlock, false AFTER it has stopped.

+    // left: adopt every queued plan and hand every queued host command back as discarded.
+    void discardPending();
```

```diff
+// GraphHost's own services first, then the embedder's.
```

### Task 7: The system nodes

`SystemNode<T>` adapts today's cores to the ABI (design section 3.1): an unlinked system is one `process()`
(prepare, step until done, finish); a linked Game Boy maps `rp.step/1` onto the same triad and `rp.link/1`
onto `linkPeers_`; outputs are zeroed and handed to `finishBlock` as its lanes, the lane count following
which outputs are connected. A node receives its core through `rp.host.cores/1`, taking it at its first
activation, and frees it (releasing its snapshot slot first) when destroyed.

The tests compare each node with an identical core driven directly, bit for bit, across the B0 block
partitions, in a new binary whose cores power on in a fixed state.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/native/src/host/graph/SystemTable.hpp`, `HostServices.hpp`, `SystemNodes.hpp`, `SystemNodes.cpp`
- Create: `packages/native/test/engine-graph/Deterministic.cpp`, `NodeRig.hpp`, `SystemNode.test.cpp`
- Modify: `packages/native/CMakeLists.txt`, `packages/native/cmake/tests.cmake`, `package.json`,
  `packages/retroplug/scripts/run-plugin-tests.mjs`

- [ ] **Step 1: The table and the services**

Create `packages/native/src/host/graph/SystemTable.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

#include "system/SystemTypes.hpp"

class SystemBase;
class SameBoySystem;
class MesenNesSystem;

namespace rp::host {

// One system as a plan sees it: its core, plus the concrete pointers the engine's per-block work needs
// (the MI.OUT collection, the NES tap), so that work needs no dynamic_cast.
struct SystemEntry {
    SystemId        id = 0;
    SystemBase*     core = nullptr;
    SameBoySystem*  sameboy = nullptr;  // null unless a Game Boy
    MesenNesSystem* nes = nullptr;      // null unless a NES
};

// The systems of one plan, in project slot order (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 4.5). Built
// on the control thread with each document and adopted with its plan, so the audio thread's lookups always
// match the plan that is running. The cores belong to the plan's system nodes, which outlive the table.
struct SystemTable {
    std::vector<SystemEntry> slots;

    // A linear scan in slot order, as Project::findSystem does.
    const SystemEntry* find(SystemId id) const {
        for (const auto& e : slots)
            if (e.id == id) return &e;
        return nullptr;
    }
};

}  // namespace rp::host
```

Create `packages/native/src/host/graph/HostServices.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The engine services B1's nodes ask the host for through rp_host.get_interface. B1 only: B2 hands
// documents to TS and B3 dissolves the kernel, and each removes the service it no longer needs.

#include <cstdint>

class SystemBase;

namespace rp::host {

// The core handoff (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 4.6). The translator parks each built
// core under a token that goes in the system node's settings; the node takes it when first activated and
// owns it from then on. [control]
#define RP_HOST_CORES_INTERFACE "rp.host.cores/1"
struct HostCores {
    void* ctx;
    // The core parked under `token`, now the caller's; null if none is (taken already, or never parked).
    SystemBase* (*take)(void* ctx, std::uint64_t token);
    // A system node is destroying its core: release what the engine keeps for it (its snapshot slot).
    void (*released)(void* ctx, SystemBase* core);
};

// The engine's kernel stage (section 3.3), run by rp.dsp.kernel as the plan's first step. [audio]
#define RP_ENGINE_KERNEL_INTERFACE "rp.engine.kernel/1"
struct EngineKernel {
    void* ctx;
    void (*run)(void* ctx, std::uint32_t frames);
};

}  // namespace rp::host
```

```cpp
// Every test in this binary compares a core run through the graph with an identical core run directly, so
// both must power on in the same state: RETROPLUG_DETERMINISTIC (util/Determinism.hpp) is read once per
// process, at the first core built, which is after this runs.

const bool kDeterministic = [] {

    return _putenv_s("RETROPLUG_DETERMINISTIC", "1") == 0;
#else
    return setenv("RETROPLUG_DETERMINISTIC", "1", 1) == 0;
#endif
}();
```

Create `packages/native/test/engine-graph/NodeRig.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// A GraphHost carrying the system node packages, with a stand-in for the engine's core handoff: a test
// parks a built core, plans a document naming its token, and the node created for it takes it.

#include <cstdio>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "graph/GraphHost.hpp"
#include "graph/HostNodes.hpp"
#include "graph/nodes/CoreNodes.hpp"
#include "host/graph/HostServices.hpp"
#include "host/graph/SystemNodes.hpp"
#include "system/SystemBase.hpp"

namespace rp::host::test {

inline std::vector<std::uint8_t> readFile(const char* path) {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes;
    if (std::FILE* f = std::fopen(path, "rb")) {
        std::fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
        bytes.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(std::ftell(f)));
        std::fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
        if (std::fread(bytes.data(), 1, bytes.size(), f) != bytes.size()) bytes.clear();
        std::fclose(f);
    }
    return bytes;
}

struct NodeRig {
    rp::graph::NodeRegistry                              registry;
    rp::graph::ResourceTable                             resources;
    std::unique_ptr<rp::graph::GraphHost>                host;
    std::map<std::uint64_t, std::unique_ptr<SystemBase>> parked;
    std::uint64_t                                        nextToken = 1;
    std::vector<SystemId>                                released;  // ids whose node freed its core
    HostCores                                            cores{this, &take, &release};

    explicit NodeRig(double sampleRate) {
        for (const rp_package* p : {rp::graph::corePackage(), rp::graph::hostPackage(), sameboyPackage(), mesenPackage()})
            registry.addPackage(p);
        rp::graph::HostConfig config;
        config.sampleRate = sampleRate;
        config.maxFrames = 8192;
        config.interfaces = [](void* ctx, const char* id) -> const void* {
            return std::string(id) == RP_HOST_CORES_INTERFACE ? &static_cast<NodeRig*>(ctx)->cores : nullptr;
        };
        config.interfacesCtx = this;
        host = std::make_unique<rp::graph::GraphHost>(registry, resources, config);
    }

    std::uint64_t park(std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> core) {
        parked.emplace(nextToken, std::move(core));
        return nextToken++;
    }

    rp::graph::SetGraphResult set(const std::string& json) { return host->setGraph(json); }

    static SystemBase* take(void* ctx, std::uint64_t token) {
        auto& p = static_cast<NodeRig*>(ctx)->parked;
        const auto it = p.find(token);
        if (it == p.end()) return nullptr;
        SystemBase* core = it->second.release();
        p.erase(it);
        return core;
    }
    static void release(void* ctx, SystemBase* core) { static_cast<NodeRig*>(ctx)->released.push_back(core->id()); }
};

}  // namespace rp::host::test
```

Create `packages/native/test/engine-graph/SystemNode.test.cpp`:

```cpp
// SystemNode<T> (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 3.1): a core run through its graph node must
// render exactly what an identical core renders driven directly through prepareForBlock /
// stepIfBelowTarget / finishBlock into zeroed lanes, bit for bit, for a lone system and a linked pair,
// across block sizes. Both cores power on in the same state (Deterministic.cpp) and hear the same input.
//
// Run via `pnpm test:plugin`.

#include <array>
#include <cstring>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>

#include "NodeRig.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenNesConfig.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenNesSystem.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenSmsConfig.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenSmsSystem.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoyConfig.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoySystem.hpp"
#include "transport/MidiTypes.hpp"

using rp::host::test::NodeRig;
using rp::host::test::readFile;

namespace {

constexpr double kRate = 48000.0;
// The B0 partitions (blocks-*.test.ts): every size, one after another, and the cycle repeats.
constexpr std::array<std::uint32_t, 5> kBlocks = {64, 199, 512, 1024, 8192};

std::unique_ptr<SameBoySystem> mgb(SystemId id) {
    SameBoyConfig cfg;
    cfg.model = SameBoyModel::DmgB;  // the DMG boot ROM plays its chime: real signal without MIDI
    cfg.fastBoot = false;
    auto sys = std::make_unique<SameBoySystem>(id, cfg, readFile(RP_MGB_ROM_PATH));
    sys->onActivate(kRate);
    return sys;
}

std::unique_ptr<MesenNesSystem> nes(SystemId id, std::uint32_t exportMode) {
    MesenNesConfig cfg;
    cfg.romPath = RP_BLIPTOASTER_ROM_PATH;
    cfg.channelExportMode = exportMode;
    auto sys = std::make_unique<MesenNesSystem>(id, cfg, readFile(RP_BLIPTOASTER_ROM_PATH));
    sys->onActivate(kRate);
    return sys;
}

std::unique_ptr<MesenSmsSystem> sms(SystemId id) {
    MesenSmsConfig cfg;
    cfg.romPath = RP_SMS_ROM_PATH;
    auto sys = std::make_unique<MesenSmsSystem>(id, cfg, readFile(RP_SMS_ROM_PATH));
    sys->onActivate(kRate);
    return sys;
}

// A note-on for the NES (through its N8 FIFO), so the mix and every pin it plays carry signal.
void noteOn(SystemBase& sys, std::uint8_t channel, std::uint8_t key) {
    MidiEvent ev;
    ev.size = 3;
    ev.data[0] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(0x90 | channel);
    ev.data[1] = key;
    ev.data[2] = 100;
    sys.onMidi(&ev, 1);
}

// BlipToaster ignores MIDI for about a second after power-on (parity/scenes.ts BOOT_MS): send both cores
// the same note once 1.5 s have rendered.
std::function<void(std::uint64_t)> afterBoot(SystemBase& a, SystemBase& b) {
    return [&a, &b, sent = false](std::uint64_t at) mutable {
        if (sent || at < static_cast<std::uint64_t>(1.5 * kRate)) return;
        noteOn(a, 0, 60);
        noteOn(b, 0, 60);
        sent = true;
    };
}

AudioBlockInfo info(std::uint32_t frames) { return AudioBlockInfo{frames, kRate, 120.0, 0.0, false}; }

// `lanes` host channels, each as long as the largest block.
struct Lanes {
    std::vector<std::vector<float>> data;
    std::vector<float*>             ptrs;
    explicit Lanes(std::size_t n) : data(n, std::vector<float>(8192, 0.0f)) {
        for (auto& d : data) ptrs.push_back(d.data());
    }
};

// Drive `direct` the way runUnit drives one unit, into `laneMap` (host channel per lane; a mono stream's
// two lanes share one), and compare with what the graph wrote to the host's channels.
struct Direct {
    std::vector<SystemBase*> members;  // in member order
    Lanes                    out;
    bool                     sounded = false;  // any block, any channel
    std::vector<std::vector<int>> laneMaps;  // per member: host channel of each finishBlock lane
    Direct(std::vector<SystemBase*> m, std::size_t channels, std::vector<std::vector<int>> maps)
        : members(std::move(m)), out(channels), laneMaps(std::move(maps)) {}

    void block(std::uint32_t frames) {
        for (auto& d : out.data) std::fill_n(d.begin(), frames, 0.0f);
        for (SystemBase* s : members) s->prepareForBlock(info(frames));
        for (bool below = true; below;) {
            below = false;
            for (SystemBase* s : members)
                if (s->stepIfBelowTarget(frames)) below = true;
        }
        for (std::size_t m = 0; m < members.size(); ++m) {
            std::vector<float*> lanes;
            for (int ch : laneMaps[m]) lanes.push_back(out.ptrs[static_cast<std::size_t>(ch)]);
            members[m]->finishBlock(info(frames), lanes.data(), lanes.size());
        }
        for (const auto& ch : out.data)
            for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < frames; ++i) sounded = sounded || ch[i] != 0.0f;
    }
};

// Renders `seconds` through both, block by block, and returns the first mismatch ("" when none). `before`
// runs ahead of each block with its first frame, to give both cores the same input.
std::string compare(NodeRig& rig, Direct& direct, double seconds, const std::function<void(std::uint64_t)>& before = {}) {
    Lanes graph(direct.out.data.size());
    const rp_transport t{kRate, 120.0, 0.0, 0};
    const auto total = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(seconds * kRate);
    std::uint64_t at = 0;
    for (std::size_t b = 0; at < total; ++b) {
        const std::uint32_t frames = kBlocks[b % kBlocks.size()];
        if (before) before(at);
        rig.host->processBlock(frames, t, graph.ptrs.data(), static_cast<std::uint32_t>(graph.ptrs.size()));
        direct.block(frames);
        for (std::size_t c = 0; c < graph.data.size(); ++c)
            if (std::memcmp(graph.data[c].data(), direct.out.data[c].data(), frames * sizeof(float)) != 0)
                return "block " + std::to_string(b) + " (" + std::to_string(frames) + " frames), channel " + std::to_string(c);
        at += frames;
    }
    return "";
}

bool sounds(const Direct& d) { return d.sounded; }

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("a lone Game Boy node renders its core's mix and stems bit for bit", "[engine-graph][system-node]") {
    SECTION("mix") {
        NodeRig rig(kRate);
        auto direct = mgb(1);
        const auto token = rig.park(mgb(1));
        REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"gb","type":"rp.sameboy.gb","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(token) +
                        R"(}},{"id":"out","type":"rp.host.audio-out","settings":{"buses":[2]}}],
                            "wires":[{"from":"gb.mix","to":"out.out.1-2"}]})").ok);
        Direct d({direct.get()}, 2, {{0, 1}});
        CHECK(compare(rig, d, 3.0) == "");
        CHECK(sounds(d));
    }
    SECTION("stems") {
        NodeRig rig(kRate);
        auto direct = mgb(1);
        const auto token = rig.park(mgb(1));
        std::string wires;
        for (int k = 1; k <= 4; ++k)
            wires += std::string(k > 1 ? "," : "") + R"({"from":"gb.ch)" + std::to_string(k) + R"(","to":"out.out.)" +
                     std::to_string(2 * k - 1) + "-" + std::to_string(2 * k) + R"("})";
        REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"gb","type":"rp.sameboy.gb","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(token) +
                        R"(}},{"id":"out","type":"rp.host.audio-out","settings":{"buses":[2,2,2,2]}}],"wires":[)" + wires + "]}")
                    .ok);
        Direct d({direct.get()}, 8, {{0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7}});
        CHECK(compare(rig, d, 3.0) == "");
        CHECK(sounds(d));
    }
}

TEST_CASE("a linked pair of Game Boys steps in runUnit's lockstep", "[engine-graph][system-node]") {
    NodeRig rig(kRate);
    auto d1 = mgb(1), d2 = mgb(2);
    d1->linkPeers_ = {d2.get()};
    d2->linkPeers_ = {d1.get()};
    const auto t1 = rig.park(mgb(1)), t2 = rig.park(mgb(2));
    REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"a","type":"rp.sameboy.gb","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(t1) +
                    R"(}},{"id":"b","type":"rp.sameboy.gb","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(t2) +
                    R"(}},{"id":"out","type":"rp.host.audio-out","settings":{"buses":[2,2]}}],
                        "wires":[{"from":"a.link","to":"b.link"},{"from":"a.mix","to":"out.out.1-2"},{"from":"b.mix","to":"out.out.3-4"}]})")
                .ok);
    Direct d({d1.get(), d2.get()}, 4, {{0, 1}, {2, 3}});
    CHECK(compare(rig, d, 3.0) == "");
    CHECK(sounds(d));
}

TEST_CASE("a lone NES node renders its mix, and its pins when tapped, bit for bit", "[engine-graph][system-node]") {
    SECTION("mix") {
        NodeRig rig(kRate);
        auto direct = nes(1, 0);
        auto viaGraph = nes(1, 0);
        SystemBase* graphCore = viaGraph.get();
        const auto token = rig.park(std::move(viaGraph));
        REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"nes","type":"rp.mesen.nes","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(token) +
                        R"(}},{"id":"out","type":"rp.host.audio-out","settings":{"buses":[2]}}],
                            "wires":[{"from":"nes.mix","to":"out.out.1-2"}]})").ok);
        Direct d({direct.get()}, 2, {{0, 1}});
        CHECK(compare(rig, d, 3.0, afterBoot(*direct, *graphCore)) == "");
        CHECK(sounds(d));
    }
    SECTION("pins") {
        NodeRig rig(kRate);
        auto direct = nes(1, 1);
        auto viaGraph = nes(1, 1);
        SystemBase* graphCore = viaGraph.get();
        const auto token = rig.park(std::move(viaGraph));
        REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"nes","type":"rp.mesen.nes","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(token) +
                        R"(}},{"id":"out","type":"rp.host.audio-out","settings":{"buses":[1,1,1]}}],
                            "wires":[{"from":"nes.pins.pulse","to":"out.out.1"},{"from":"nes.pins.tnd","to":"out.out.2"},
                                     {"from":"nes.pins.expansion","to":"out.out.3"}]})").ok);
        Direct d({direct.get()}, 3, {{0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2}});  // a mono stream's two lanes are one buffer
        CHECK(compare(rig, d, 3.0, afterBoot(*direct, *graphCore)) == "");
        CHECK(sounds(d));
    }
}

TEST_CASE("a lone SMS node renders its core's mix bit for bit", "[engine-graph][system-node]") {
    NodeRig rig(kRate);
    auto direct = sms(1);
    const auto token = rig.park(sms(1));
    REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"sms","type":"rp.mesen.sms","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(token) +
                    R"(}},{"id":"out","type":"rp.host.audio-out","settings":{"buses":[2]}}],
                        "wires":[{"from":"sms.mix","to":"out.out.1-2"}]})").ok);
    Direct d({direct.get()}, 2, {{0, 1}});
    CHECK(compare(rig, d, 2.0) == "");
}

TEST_CASE("a system node takes its core once, and releases it when destroyed", "[engine-graph][system-node]") {
    NodeRig rig(kRate);
    const auto token = rig.park(mgb(7));
    const std::string doc = R"({"nodes":[{"id":"gb","type":"rp.sameboy.gb","settings":{"core":)" + std::to_string(token) + "}}]," +
                            R"("wires":[]})";
    REQUIRE(rig.set(doc).ok);
    CHECK(rig.parked.empty());
    REQUIRE(rig.set(doc).ok);  // the same document reuses the instance and its core
    CHECK(rig.released.empty());
    REQUIRE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[],"wires":[]})").ok);
    rig.host->reclaim();
    CHECK(rig.released == std::vector<SystemId>{7});
    // A token nothing is parked under does not plan.
    CHECK_FALSE(rig.set(R"({"nodes":[{"id":"gb","type":"rp.sameboy.gb","settings":{"core":999}}],"wires":[]})").ok);
}
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "graph/rp_node.h"

namespace rp::host {

// The system node packages (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 3.1): rp.sameboy.gb, and
// rp.mesen.nes / .gba / .sms. Each node adapts one of today's SystemBase cores; it needs the engine's
```

Create `packages/native/src/host/graph/SystemNodes.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "host/graph/SystemNodes.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cstring>
#include <iterator>
#include <span>
#include <string>

#include "graph/node.hpp"
#include "host/graph/HostServices.hpp"
#include "system/SystemBase.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenGbaSystem.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenNesSystem.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenSmsSystem.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoySystem.hpp"

namespace rp::host {

// Named, so its JSON Schema $defs key is the same under every compiler.
struct SystemNodeSettings {
    // The token the translator parked this node's built core under (section 4.6). A new core is a new
    // token, so it is always a new instance.
    std::uint64_t core = 0;
};

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxOutputs = 16;

// What every system node shares: the core handoff, the connected-output mask, and prepare / step / finish
// mapped one to one onto the core's block triad. `Self` supplies finish's lanes.
template <class Self, class Core>
struct SystemNode {
    using Settings = SystemNodeSettings;
    static bool needs_rebuild(const Settings& a, const Settings& b) { return a.core != b.core; }

    const HostCores*                       cores = nullptr;
    std::uint64_t                          token = 0;
    Core*                                  core = nullptr;  // owned
    std::array<std::uint8_t, kMaxOutputs>  connected{};

    SystemNode() = default;
    SystemNode(const SystemNode&) = delete;
    SystemNode& operator=(const SystemNode&) = delete;
    // Destroyed at reclaim, on the control thread, once no plan runs it: today's reclaimReleased order.
    ~SystemNode() {
        if (!core) return;
        if (cores) cores->released(cores->ctx, core);
        delete core;
    }

    void bind_host(const rp_host* h) noexcept {
        cores = static_cast<const HostCores*>(h->get_interface(h, RP_HOST_CORES_INTERFACE));
    }
    void on_settings(const Settings& s) noexcept { token = s.core; }

    // The first activation takes the core; later ones (a re-activation) keep it. The factory activated the
    // core at the engine's rate when it built it, and Engine::setSampleRate re-rates live cores itself.
    bool activate(double, std::uint32_t) {
        if (core) return true;
        if (!cores) return false;
        SystemBase* built = cores->take(cores->ctx, token);
        core = dynamic_cast<Core*>(built);
        if (!core) delete built;  // a token naming another kind of core is a translator bug
        return core != nullptr;
    }

    void ports_changed(std::span<const std::uint8_t> mask) noexcept {
        connected.fill(0);
        std::copy_n(mask.begin(), std::min(mask.size(), connected.size()), connected.begin());
    }

    static AudioBlockInfo info(const rp::Block& b) {
        const rp_transport& t = b.transport();
        return AudioBlockInfo{b.frames(), t.sample_rate, t.tempo, t.ppq, (t.flags & RP_TRANSPORT_PLAYING) != 0};
    }

    // An unlinked system: prepare, step until it has the block, finish - runUnit for one member.
    void process(const rp::Block& b) noexcept {
        prepare(b);
        while (core->stepIfBelowTarget(b.frames())) {
        }
        finish(b);
    }
    void prepare(const rp::Block& b) noexcept { core->prepareForBlock(info(b)); }
    bool step(std::uint32_t target) noexcept { return !core->stepIfBelowTarget(target); }

    // finishBlock adds into its lanes, so they start at zero, as today's zeroed bus does. Every other
    // connected output is written too (the ABI's rule), as silence.
    void finish(const rp::Block& b) noexcept {
        std::array<float*, 2 * kMaxOutputs> lanes{};
        const std::size_t n = static_cast<Self*>(this)->lanes(b, lanes);
        for (std::uint32_t o = 0; o < b.raw.output_count && o < connected.size(); ++o)
            if (connected[o])
                for (std::uint32_t c = 0; c < b.output(o).u.audio.channel_count; ++c)
                    std::memset(b.output(o).u.audio.channels[c], 0, b.frames() * sizeof(float));
        for (std::size_t l = 0; l < n; ++l) std::memset(lanes[l], 0, b.frames() * sizeof(float));
        core->finishBlock(info(b), lanes.data(), n);
    }

    // The stereo mix as lanes 0 and 1.
    static std::size_t mixLanes(const rp::Block& b, std::array<float*, 2 * kMaxOutputs>& lanes) {
        lanes[0] = b.output(0).u.audio.channels[0];
        lanes[1] = b.output(0).u.audio.channels[1];
        return 2;
    }
};

struct GameBoy : SystemNode<GameBoy, SameBoySystem> {
    static constexpr const char* id = "rp.sameboy.gb";
    static constexpr const char* name = "Game Boy";
    static constexpr const char* category = "system";
    struct { rp::link link; } inputs;
    struct {
        rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> mix;
        rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> ch1;
        rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> ch2;
        rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> ch3;
        rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> ch4;
    } outputs;

    // The four stems (8 lanes) once any is connected, else the mix (2): finishBlock's two lane counts.
    std::size_t lanes(const rp::Block& b, std::array<float*, 2 * kMaxOutputs>& out) const {
        if (!(connected[1] || connected[2] || connected[3] || connected[4])) return mixLanes(b, out);
        for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < 4; ++k) {
            out[2 * k] = b.output(1 + k).u.audio.channels[0];
            out[2 * k + 1] = b.output(1 + k).u.audio.channels[1];
        }
        return 8;
    }

    // The coupled group's other members, in member order: what rebuildLinkGroups puts in linkPeers_.
    // linkPeers_ reserves 8, so a cable of up to 9 Game Boys never allocates here.
    void set_peers(std::span<rp_node* const> peers) noexcept {
        core->linkPeers_.clear();
        for (rp_node* p : peers)
            if (GameBoy* gb = rp::peer_as<GameBoy>(p)) core->linkPeers_.push_back(gb->core);
    }
};

// Output order: mix, then the tap ports by the mode that drives them.
constexpr const char* kNesTaps[] = {"pins.pulse",  "pins.tnd",     "pins.expansion", "pins.mixref",   "channels.sq1",
                                    "channels.sq2", "channels.tri", "channels.noise", "channels.dmc"};
constexpr std::uint32_t kPinsFirst = 1;      // output index of pins.pulse
constexpr std::uint32_t kChannelsFirst = 5;  // output index of channels.sq1

struct Nes : SystemNode<Nes, MesenNesSystem> {
    static constexpr const char* id = "rp.mesen.nes";
    static constexpr const char* name = "NES";
    static constexpr const char* category = "system";
    struct { rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> mix; } outputs;

    static void adjust_layout(rp::graph::Layout& l, const Settings&) {
        using namespace rp::graph;
        for (const char* port : kNesTaps) l.ports.push_back({port, PortDir::Out, PortKind::Audio, ChannelSpec::fixed(1, "mono"), {}, false});
        l.exclusive.push_back({"pins.*", "channels.*"});
    }

    // With mix connected, the mix (2 lanes), whether or not the tap is armed: the narrow-host defect's
    // path. Otherwise the armed mode's streams, each mono stream's two lanes one buffer, as
    // ChannelSplitRouter hands a mono stream today (the NES writes only lane 2k).
    std::size_t lanes(const rp::Block& b, std::array<float*, 2 * kMaxOutputs>& out) const {
        bool tap = false;
        for (std::uint32_t o = kPinsFirst; o < 1 + std::size(kNesTaps); ++o) tap = tap || connected[o];
        const std::uint32_t mode = core->channelExportMode();
        const std::uint32_t streams = mode == 1 ? 3 : mode == 2 ? 4 : mode == 3 ? 5 : 0;
        if (connected[0] || !tap || streams == 0) return mixLanes(b, out);
        const std::uint32_t first = mode == 3 ? kChannelsFirst : kPinsFirst;
        for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < streams; ++k) out[2 * k] = out[2 * k + 1] = b.output(first + k).u.audio.channels[0];
        return 2 * streams;
    }
};

struct Gba : SystemNode<Gba, MesenGbaSystem> {
    static constexpr const char* id = "rp.mesen.gba";
    static constexpr const char* name = "Game Boy Advance";
    static constexpr const char* category = "system";
    struct { rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> mix; } outputs;
    std::size_t lanes(const rp::Block& b, std::array<float*, 2 * kMaxOutputs>& out) const { return mixLanes(b, out); }
};

struct Sms : SystemNode<Sms, MesenSmsSystem> {
    static constexpr const char* id = "rp.mesen.sms";
    static constexpr const char* name = "Master System / Game Gear";
    static constexpr const char* category = "system";
    struct { rp::audio_out<rp::stereo> mix; } outputs;
    std::size_t lanes(const rp::Block& b, std::array<float*, 2 * kMaxOutputs>& out) const { return mixLanes(b, out); }
};

}  // namespace

const rp_package* sameboyPackage() {
    static const rp_node_type* const kTypes[] = {rp::node_type<GameBoy>()};
    static const rp_package kPackage{RP_ABI_VERSION, R"({"id":"rp.sameboy","version":"1.0.0"})",
                                     static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::size(kTypes)), kTypes};
    return &kPackage;
}

const rp_package* mesenPackage() {
    static const rp_node_type* const kTypes[] = {rp::node_type<Nes>(), rp::node_type<Gba>(), rp::node_type<Sms>()};
    static const rp_package kPackage{RP_ABI_VERSION, R"({"id":"rp.mesen","version":"1.0.0"})",
                                     static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::size(kTypes)), kTypes};
    return &kPackage;
}

}  // namespace rp::host
```

`retroplug-backend` compiles them and links the graph library, in `packages/native/CMakeLists.txt` (the
sources line for `KernelNode.cpp` and `Translator.cpp` arrives with Task 8):

```cmake
    src/host/graph/SystemNodes.cpp          # graph mode: today's cores as rp.sameboy.gb / rp.mesen.* nodes
```

```cmake
target_link_libraries(retroplug-backend PUBLIC
    retroplug-core      # Project + SystemBase + SameBoy/Mesen; PUBLIC-brings rpcpp + miniz
    retroplug-graph     # the graph executor, planner and GraphHost the engine runs on in graph mode
```

### Task 8: The kernel node and the translator

`rp.dsp.kernel` runs the engine's kernel stage through `rp.engine.kernel/1` (design section 3.3). The
translator (section 4) holds the structural model and builds each document and its system table: document
order is the kernel, unlinked systems by slot, link groups, mixing, `audio-out`; each host channel is fed
the contributions today's router adds into it, in today's order. It takes a `Publish` callback rather than
the GraphHost, so its documents can be tested without planning them.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/native/src/host/graph/KernelNode.{hpp,cpp}`, `Translator.{hpp,cpp}`
- Create: `packages/native/test/engine-graph/Translator.test.cpp`
- Modify: `packages/native/CMakeLists.txt`, `packages/native/cmake/tests.cmake` (the `Translator.test.cpp` line)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `packages/native/test/engine-graph/Translator.test.cpp`:

```cpp
// The translator (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 4): today's structural calls as documents.
// Each host channel must be fed what today's router adds into it, in today's order, and systems must run
// in runBlock's order. The documents are captured, not planned, so most systems here are stand-ins that
// report a kind; the link-group cases need real Game Boys (the link group is SameBoy config).
//
// Run via `pnpm test:plugin`.

#include <algorithm>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>

#include "NodeRig.hpp"
#include "graph/GraphDocument.hpp"
#include "host/graph/Translator.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoyConfig.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoySystem.hpp"

using rp::host::HostShape;
using rp::host::Translator;
using rp::host::test::readFile;

namespace {

class StandIn final : public SystemBase {
public:
    StandIn(SystemId id, SystemKind k) : SystemBase(id), kind_(k) {}
    SystemKind kind() const override { return kind_; }
    void onActivate(double) override {}
    void onSampleRateChanged(double) override {}
    std::vector<ChannelStream> channelLayout() const override { return {{"Mix", true}}; }
    void finishBlock(const AudioBlockInfo&, float* const*, std::size_t) override {}

private:
    SystemKind kind_;
};

std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> gb(SystemId id) { return std::make_unique<StandIn>(id, SystemKind::SameBoy); }
std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> nes(SystemId id) { return std::make_unique<StandIn>(id, SystemKind::MesenNes); }

std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> linkedMgb(SystemId id, std::uint8_t group) {
    SameBoyConfig cfg;
    cfg.linkGroupId = group;
    return std::make_unique<SameBoySystem>(id, cfg, readFile(RP_MGB_ROM_PATH));
}

struct Rig {
    std::vector<std::string> docs;
    std::vector<SystemId>    released;
    bool                     accept = true;
    Translator               t{[this](const std::string& json, std::shared_ptr<const rp::host::SystemTable>) {
                     docs.push_back(json);
                     return accept;
                 },
                 [this](SystemBase* core) { released.push_back(core->id()); }};

    rp::graph::GraphDocument doc() const { return *rp::graph::parseGraphDocument(docs.back()); }

    std::vector<std::string> nodeOrder() const {
        std::vector<std::string> out;
        for (const auto& n : doc().nodes) out.push_back(n.id);
        return out;
    }

    std::string from(const std::string& to) const {
        for (const auto& w : doc().wires)
            if (w.to == to) return w.from;
        return "";
    }

    // What feeds host channel `c` (0-based), in order: the one endpoint wired straight in, or every input of
    // the rp.core.add chain that sums into it.
    std::vector<std::string> lane(std::uint32_t c) const {
        std::vector<std::string> out;
        std::string src = from("out.out." + std::to_string(c + 1));
        if (src.rfind("lane-", 0) != 0) return src.empty() ? out : std::vector<std::string>{src};
        expand(src.substr(0, src.find('.')), out);
        return out;
    }
    void expand(const std::string& add, std::vector<std::string>& out) const {
        for (int k = 1;; ++k) {
            const std::string in = from(add + ".in" + std::to_string(k));
            if (in.empty()) return;
            if (in.rfind("lane-", 0) == 0) expand(in.substr(0, in.find('.')), out);
            else out.push_back(in);
        }
    }

    std::vector<std::uint32_t> buses() const {
        const auto d = doc();
        for (const auto& n : d.nodes) {
            if (n.id != "out") continue;
            const auto object = n.settings->to_object();
            const auto array = object->get("buses")->to_array();
            std::vector<std::uint32_t> b;
            for (const auto& v : *array) b.push_back(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(*v.to_int()));
            return b;
        }
        return {};
    }
};

using V = std::vector<std::string>;

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("the empty project is the kernel and the host's outputs", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.docs.size() == 1);
    CHECK(r.nodeOrder() == V{"kernel", "out"});
    CHECK(r.buses() == std::vector<std::uint32_t>{1, 1});
}

TEST_CASE("Stereo sums every system into one pair, in slot order", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(1)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(nes(2)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(3)));
    CHECK(r.nodeOrder() == V{"kernel", "sys-1", "sys-2", "sys-3", "split-1", "split-2", "split-3", "lane-0", "lane-1", "out"});
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1", "split-2.out1", "split-3.out1"});
    CHECK(r.lane(1) == V{"split-1.out2", "split-2.out2", "split-3.out2"});
    REQUIRE(r.t.remove(2));  // a remove shifts the later slots down
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1", "split-3.out1"});
}

TEST_CASE("one system on a lane is wired straight in", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(1)));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1"});
    CHECK(r.from("out.out.1") == "split-1.out1");
}

TEST_CASE("OnePerInstance puts L then R on one lane, and wraps past the outputs", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    for (SystemId id = 1; id <= 3; ++id) REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(id)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setRouting(AudioRouting::OnePerInstance));
    // Flat, never (L0 + R0) + (L2 + R2): float addition is not associative.
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1", "split-1.out2", "split-3.out1", "split-3.out2"});
    CHECK(r.lane(1) == V{"split-2.out1", "split-2.out2"});
}

TEST_CASE("TwoPerInstance gives slot i the pair 2i mod N", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    for (SystemId id = 1; id <= 5; ++id) REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(id)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setRouting(AudioRouting::TwoPerInstance));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::Multi, 4}));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1", "split-3.out1", "split-5.out1"});
    CHECK(r.lane(1) == V{"split-1.out2", "split-3.out2", "split-5.out2"});
    CHECK(r.lane(2) == V{"split-2.out1", "split-4.out1"});
    CHECK(r.lane(3) == V{"split-2.out2", "split-4.out2"});
}

TEST_CASE("a lane fed by more than 16 contributions chains its adds", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    for (SystemId id = 1; id <= 20; ++id) REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(id)));
    V want;
    for (SystemId id = 1; id <= 20; ++id) want.push_back("split-" + std::to_string(id) + ".out1");
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == want);
    CHECK(r.from("lane-0-1.in1") == "lane-0.out");
}

TEST_CASE("linked Game Boys run after the unlinked ones, each group in slot order", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(linkedMgb(1, 1)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(linkedMgb(2, 0)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(linkedMgb(3, 1)));
    CHECK(r.nodeOrder() == V{"kernel", "sys-2", "sys-1", "sys-3", "split-2", "split-1", "split-3", "lane-0", "lane-1", "out"});
    CHECK(r.from("sys-3.link") == "sys-1.link");
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-2.out1", "split-1.out1", "split-3.out1"});
    REQUIRE(r.t.setLinkGroup(3, 0));  // unlinking dissolves a group of two
    CHECK(r.nodeOrder() == V{"kernel", "sys-1", "sys-2", "sys-3", "split-1", "split-2", "split-3", "lane-0", "lane-1", "out"});
    CHECK(r.from("sys-3.link").empty());
}

TEST_CASE("ChannelSplit fans a Game Boy's stems, or falls back to Stereo on a narrow host", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(1)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setRouting(AudioRouting::ChannelSplit));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::Multi, 8}));
    for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < 4; ++k) {
        const std::string stem = "stem-1-" + std::to_string(k);
        CHECK(r.from(stem + ".in") == "sys-1.ch" + std::to_string(k + 1));
        CHECK(r.lane(2 * k) == V{stem + ".out1"});
        CHECK(r.lane(2 * k + 1) == V{stem + ".out2"});
    }
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::Multi, 4}));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1"});
    CHECK(r.lane(2).empty());
}

TEST_CASE("a single NES splits into channels, pins, or pins on pairs", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(nes(1)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::Multi, 8}));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setRouting(AudioRouting::ChannelSplit));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"sys-1.channels.sq1"});
    CHECK(r.lane(4) == V{"sys-1.channels.dmc"});
    CHECK(r.lane(5).empty());
    REQUIRE(r.t.setRouting(AudioRouting::PinSplit));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"sys-1.pins.pulse"});
    CHECK(r.lane(2) == V{"sys-1.pins.expansion"});
    REQUIRE(r.t.setRouting(AudioRouting::StereoPinSplit));
    CHECK(r.lane(2) == V{"sys-1.pins.tnd"});  // each pin mirrored onto both lanes of its pair
    CHECK(r.lane(3) == V{"sys-1.pins.tnd"});
    // Too narrow for its lanes: the mix feeds the host as in Stereo (the tap is the engine's SyncTap).
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::Multi, 4}));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1"});
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(2)));  // two systems: every split renders as Stereo
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::Multi, 8}));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-1.out1", "split-2.out1"});
}

TEST_CASE("PerSystem and PerChannel lay the shapes the render calls want", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(1)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(nes(2)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::PerSystem, 0}));
    CHECK(r.from("out.out.1-2") == "sys-1.mix");
    CHECK(r.from("out.out.3-4") == "sys-2.mix");
    CHECK(r.buses() == std::vector<std::uint32_t>{2, 2});
    REQUIRE(r.t.remove(1));
    REQUIRE(r.t.setRouting(AudioRouting::ChannelSplit));  // arms the NES's five channels
    REQUIRE(r.t.setShape({HostShape::Kind::PerChannel, 0}));
    CHECK(r.from("out.out.1") == "sys-2.channels.sq1");
    CHECK(r.from("out.out.9") == "sys-2.channels.dmc");
    CHECK(r.buses() == std::vector<std::uint32_t>(10, 1));
}

TEST_CASE("replace keeps the slot; a refused plan leaves the model as it was", "[engine-graph][translator]") {
    Rig r;
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(1)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.add(gb(2)));
    REQUIRE(r.t.replace(1, gb(5)));
    CHECK(r.lane(0) == V{"split-5.out1", "split-2.out1"});
    CHECK_FALSE(r.t.replace(9, gb(6)));  // no such system: the core is freed, never adopted
    CHECK(r.released == std::vector<SystemId>{6});
    r.accept = false;
    CHECK_FALSE(r.t.add(gb(7)));
    CHECK(r.released == std::vector<SystemId>{6, 7});
    CHECK(r.t.systemCount() == 2);
}
```

```cpp
#include "host/graph/KernelNode.hpp"

#include <iterator>

#include "graph/node.hpp"
#include "host/graph/HostServices.hpp"

namespace rp::host {

namespace {

struct Kernel {
    static constexpr const char* id = "rp.dsp.kernel";
    static constexpr const char* name = "DSP Kernel";
    static constexpr const char* category = "dsp";

    const EngineKernel* kernel = nullptr;

    void bind_host(const rp_host* h) noexcept {
        kernel = static_cast<const EngineKernel*>(h->get_interface(h, RP_ENGINE_KERNEL_INTERFACE));
    }
    bool activate(double, std::uint32_t) { return kernel != nullptr; }
    void process(const rp::Block& b) noexcept { kernel->run(kernel->ctx, b.frames()); }
};

}  // namespace

const rp_package* dspPackage() {
    static const rp_node_type* const kTypes[] = {rp::node_type<Kernel>()};
    static const rp_package kPackage{RP_ABI_VERSION, R"({"id":"rp.dsp","version":"1.0.0"})",
                                     static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::size(kTypes)), kTypes};
    return &kPackage;
}

}  // namespace rp::host
```
