- [ ] **Step 4: The translator**

Create `packages/native/src/host/graph/Translator.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "host/graph/SystemTable.hpp"
#include "system/AudioRouting.hpp"
#include "system/SystemTypes.hpp"

class SystemBase;

namespace rp::host {

// The output shape a document is built for (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 4.4): the host's
// own outputs routed by the project's mode (Multi), each system on its own pair (PerSystem), or one
// system's streams on a pair each (PerChannel).
struct HostShape {
    enum class Kind : std::uint8_t { Multi, PerSystem, PerChannel };
    Kind          kind = Kind::Multi;
    std::uint32_t channels = 2;  // Multi only: the host's output count
    bool operator==(const HostShape&) const = default;
};

// Turns today's structural calls into graph documents (section 4). Control thread only. It mirrors
// Project: the ordered system list, each Game Boy's link group, the routing mode and the host shape, and
// after every change it builds the document and its system table and hands both to `publish` (the
// engine's GraphHost::setGraph), which answers whether the document planned.
//
// A built core is parked here under a token until the system node created for it takes it (take()). A
// core it frees itself (one never adopted) goes through `released` first, as a node's destroy does.
class Translator {
public:
    using Publish = std::function<bool(const std::string& documentJson, std::shared_ptr<const SystemTable> table)>;
    Translator(Publish publish, std::function<void(SystemBase*)> released);
    ~Translator();  // frees any core still parked
    Translator(const Translator&) = delete;
    Translator& operator=(const Translator&) = delete;

    // Each returns false when the change could not be planned (the model is then left as it was, and a
    // core that was handed in is freed).
    bool add(std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> core);
    bool replace(SystemId id, std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> core);  // keeps id's slot; false if id is absent
    bool remove(SystemId id);
    bool setRouting(AudioRouting mode);
    bool setLinkGroup(SystemId id, std::uint8_t group);  // a no-op when unchanged, as today
    bool setShape(HostShape shape);                        // a no-op when unchanged

    AudioRouting routing() const { return routing_; }
    const HostShape& shape() const { return shape_; }
    std::size_t  systemCount() const { return systems_.size(); }
    // The core of `id`, for the control-thread readers that may touch a core only while no audio thread
    // runs (the debug facet, the per-channel render's layout). Null for an unknown id.
    SystemBase*  find(SystemId id) const;
    // Every core, in slot order (a sample-rate change re-rates them, quiescent only).
    std::vector<SystemBase*> cores() const;

    // rp.host.cores/1: the core parked under `token`, now the caller's.
    SystemBase* take(std::uint64_t token);

    // What the model plans as: the document's JSON and the table that rides with it. Public for tests.
    std::string documentJson() const;
    SystemTable table() const;

private:
    struct System {
        SystemId      id = 0;
        SystemKind    kind = SystemKind::SameBoy;
        SystemBase*   core = nullptr;  // parked here, or owned by its node
        std::uint64_t token = 0;
        std::uint8_t  linkGroup = 0;   // Game Boys only; 0 = unlinked
        std::uint32_t buildMode = 0;   // NES only: the channel export mode it was built with
    };

    System entryFor(std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> core);
    void   unpark(std::uint64_t token);  // free a parked core the plan never took
    bool   publish();

    // The order systems are summed and stepped in: unlinked by slot, then each link group (by first
    // appearance) with its members by slot. Indices into systems_.
    std::vector<std::size_t>              sumOrder() const;
    std::vector<std::vector<std::size_t>> linkGroups() const;
    // The export mode a NES has once today's syncSplitPlan has armed or restored it.
    std::uint32_t                         nesMode(const System& s) const;

    Publish                                            publish_;
    std::function<void(SystemBase*)>                   released_;
    std::vector<System>                                systems_;  // slot order
    std::map<std::uint64_t, std::unique_ptr<SystemBase>> parked_;
    std::uint64_t                                      nextToken_ = 1;
    AudioRouting                                       routing_ = AudioRouting::Stereo;
    HostShape                                          shape_;
};

}  // namespace rp::host
```

Create `packages/native/src/host/graph/Translator.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "host/graph/Translator.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <string>

#include "graph/CanonicalJson.hpp"
#include "system/SystemBase.hpp"
#include "system/mesen/MesenNesSystem.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoySystem.hpp"

namespace rp::host {

namespace {

// rp.core.add's input limit (CoreNodes.cpp): a longer sum chains adds, which continues the same
// left-to-right sum.
constexpr std::size_t kAddInputs = 16;

std::string sysNode(SystemId id) { return "sys-" + std::to_string(id); }

const char* typeOf(SystemKind k) {
    switch (k) {
        case SystemKind::SameBoy: return "rp.sameboy.gb";
        case SystemKind::MesenNes: return "rp.mesen.nes";
        case SystemKind::MesenGba: return "rp.mesen.gba";
        case SystemKind::MesenSms: return "rp.mesen.sms";
    }
    return "";
}

// One output stream of a system, as channelLayout() reports it: its port on the system node.
struct Stream {
    std::string port;
    bool        stereo = true;
};

// The streams a core reports in `mode` (a NES's export mode; ignored otherwise).
std::vector<Stream> streamsOf(SystemKind kind, std::uint32_t mode) {
    switch (kind) {
        case SystemKind::SameBoy: return {{"ch1"}, {"ch2"}, {"ch3"}, {"ch4"}};
        case SystemKind::MesenNes:
            if (mode == 1) return {{"pins.pulse", false}, {"pins.tnd", false}, {"pins.expansion", false}};
            if (mode == 2) return {{"pins.pulse", false}, {"pins.tnd", false}, {"pins.expansion", false}, {"pins.mixref", false}};
            if (mode == 3)
                return {{"channels.sq1", false}, {"channels.sq2", false}, {"channels.tri", false}, {"channels.noise", false},
                        {"channels.dmc", false}};
            return {{"mix"}};
        default: return {{"mix"}};
    }
}

// rp.host.audio-out's port for the bus starting at host channel `first` (0-based), `n` channels wide.
std::string outPort(std::uint32_t first, std::uint32_t n) {
    return "out.out." + std::to_string(first + 1) + (n == 1 ? "" : "-" + std::to_string(first + n));
}

// The document as JSON, in the order it is built: node order is plan order on ties (section 4.2).
struct Doc {
    std::vector<std::string> nodes;
    std::vector<std::string> wires;
    void node(const std::string& id, const char* type, const std::string& settings = "") {
        nodes.push_back(R"({"id":)" + rp::graph::jsonString(id) + R"(,"type":)" + rp::graph::jsonString(type) +
                        (settings.empty() ? "" : R"(,"settings":)" + settings) + "}");
    }
    void wire(const std::string& from, const std::string& to) {
        wires.push_back(R"({"from":)" + rp::graph::jsonString(from) + R"(,"to":)" + rp::graph::jsonString(to) + "}");
    }
    // A stereo endpoint as its two mono halves, through an rp.core.split named `name`.
    std::pair<std::string, std::string> halves(const std::string& from, const std::string& name) {
        node(name, "rp.core.split");
        wire(from, name + ".in");
        return {name + ".out1", name + ".out2"};
    }
    std::string json() const {
        std::string s = R"({"nodes":[)";
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < nodes.size(); ++i) s += (i ? "," : "") + nodes[i];
        s += R"(],"wires":[)";
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < wires.size(); ++i) s += (i ? "," : "") + wires[i];
        return s + "]}";
    }
};

}  // namespace

Translator::Translator(Publish publish, std::function<void(SystemBase*)> released)
    : publish_(std::move(publish)), released_(std::move(released)) {
    this->publish();  // the empty project: the kernel and the host's outputs
}

Translator::~Translator() {
    for (auto& [token, core] : parked_)
        if (released_) released_(core.get());
}

Translator::System Translator::entryFor(std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> core) {
    System s;
    s.id = core->id();
    s.kind = core->kind();
    s.core = core.get();
    s.token = nextToken_++;
    if (auto* sb = dynamic_cast<SameBoySystem*>(core.get())) s.linkGroup = sb->config_.linkGroupId;
    if (auto* nes = dynamic_cast<MesenNesSystem*>(core.get())) s.buildMode = nes->channelExportMode();
    parked_.emplace(s.token, std::move(core));
    return s;
}

void Translator::unpark(std::uint64_t token) {
    const auto it = parked_.find(token);
    if (it == parked_.end()) return;  // the plan took it, and its node freed it
    if (released_) released_(it->second.get());
    parked_.erase(it);
}

SystemBase* Translator::take(std::uint64_t token) {
    const auto it = parked_.find(token);
    if (it == parked_.end()) return nullptr;
    SystemBase* core = it->second.release();
    parked_.erase(it);
    return core;
}

bool Translator::add(std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> core) {
    if (!core) return false;
    systems_.push_back(entryFor(std::move(core)));
    if (publish()) return true;
    const std::uint64_t token = systems_.back().token;
    systems_.pop_back();
    unpark(token);
    return false;
}

bool Translator::replace(SystemId id, std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> core) {
    if (!core) return false;
    const auto it = std::find_if(systems_.begin(), systems_.end(), [&](const System& s) { return s.id == id; });
    if (it == systems_.end()) {
        // Never adopted, as Project::swapSystem hands back a core whose target is gone.
        if (released_) released_(core.get());
        return false;
    }
    const System old = *it;
    *it = entryFor(std::move(core));
    if (publish()) return true;
    const std::uint64_t token = it->token;
    *it = old;
    unpark(token);
    return false;
}

bool Translator::remove(SystemId id) {
    const auto it = std::find_if(systems_.begin(), systems_.end(), [&](const System& s) { return s.id == id; });
    if (it == systems_.end()) return true;
    const System old = *it;
    const auto at = systems_.erase(it);
    if (publish()) return true;
    systems_.insert(at, old);
    return false;
}

bool Translator::setRouting(AudioRouting mode) {
    const AudioRouting old = routing_;
    routing_ = mode;
    if (publish()) return true;
    routing_ = old;
    return false;
}

bool Translator::setLinkGroup(SystemId id, std::uint8_t group) {
    const auto it = std::find_if(systems_.begin(), systems_.end(), [&](const System& s) { return s.id == id; });
    if (it == systems_.end() || it->kind != SystemKind::SameBoy || it->linkGroup == group) return true;
    const std::uint8_t old = it->linkGroup;
    it->linkGroup = group;
    if (publish()) return true;
    it->linkGroup = old;
    return false;
}

bool Translator::setShape(HostShape shape) {
    if (shape == shape_) return true;
    const HostShape old = shape_;
    shape_ = shape;
    if (publish()) return true;
    shape_ = old;
    return false;
}

SystemBase* Translator::find(SystemId id) const {
    for (const auto& s : systems_)
        if (s.id == id) return s.core;
    return nullptr;
}

std::vector<SystemBase*> Translator::cores() const {
    std::vector<SystemBase*> out;
    for (const auto& s : systems_) out.push_back(s.core);
    return out;
}

// Project::rebuildLinkGroups: Game Boys with a nonzero group, bucketed in slot order, groups of fewer than
// two dissolved.
std::vector<std::vector<std::size_t>> Translator::linkGroups() const {
    std::vector<std::pair<std::uint8_t, std::vector<std::size_t>>> groups;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < systems_.size(); ++i) {
        const System& s = systems_[i];
        if (s.kind != SystemKind::SameBoy || s.linkGroup == 0) continue;
        auto g = std::find_if(groups.begin(), groups.end(), [&](const auto& e) { return e.first == s.linkGroup; });
        if (g == groups.end()) groups.push_back({s.linkGroup, {i}});
        else g->second.push_back(i);
    }
    std::vector<std::vector<std::size_t>> out;
    for (auto& g : groups)
        if (g.second.size() >= 2) out.push_back(std::move(g.second));
    return out;
}

std::vector<std::size_t> Translator::sumOrder() const {
    const auto groups = linkGroups();
    std::vector<bool> linked(systems_.size(), false);
    for (const auto& g : groups)
        for (std::size_t i : g) linked[i] = true;
    std::vector<std::size_t> order;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < systems_.size(); ++i)
        if (!linked[i]) order.push_back(i);
    for (const auto& g : groups) order.insert(order.end(), g.begin(), g.end());
    return order;
}

std::uint32_t Translator::nesMode(const System& s) const {
    const bool pins = routing_ == AudioRouting::PinSplit || routing_ == AudioRouting::StereoPinSplit;
    const bool split = pins || routing_ == AudioRouting::ChannelSplit;
    if (split && systems_.size() == 1 && s.kind == SystemKind::MesenNes) return pins ? 1u : 3u;
    return s.buildMode;
}

std::string Translator::documentJson() const {
    Doc d;
    d.node("kernel", "rp.dsp.kernel");
    const std::vector<std::size_t> order = sumOrder();
    for (std::size_t i : order)
        d.node(sysNode(systems_[i].id), typeOf(systems_[i].kind), R"({"core":)" + std::to_string(systems_[i].token) + "}");
    for (const auto& g : linkGroups())
        for (std::size_t k = 1; k < g.size(); ++k)
            d.wire(sysNode(systems_[g[k - 1]].id) + ".link", sysNode(systems_[g[k]].id) + ".link");

    std::vector<std::uint32_t> buses;
    if (shape_.kind == HostShape::Kind::PerSystem) {
        // PerSystemRouter: slot i's mix on channels 2i and 2i + 1.
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < systems_.size(); ++i) {
            d.wire(sysNode(systems_[i].id) + ".mix", outPort(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(2 * i), 2));
            buses.push_back(2);
        }
    } else if (shape_.kind == HostShape::Kind::PerChannel) {
        // PerChannelRouter: one system's stream s on channels 2s and 2s + 1; a mono stream fills only 2s.
        if (systems_.size() == 1) {
            const System& s = systems_[0];
            const auto streams = streamsOf(s.kind, nesMode(s));
            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < streams.size(); ++k) {
                const std::uint32_t first = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(2 * k);
                if (streams[k].stereo) {
                    d.wire(sysNode(s.id) + "." + streams[k].port, outPort(first, 2));
                    buses.push_back(2);
                } else {
                    d.wire(sysNode(s.id) + "." + streams[k].port, outPort(first, 1));
                    buses.push_back(1);
                    buses.push_back(1);
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        const std::uint32_t n = shape_.channels;
        std::vector<std::vector<std::string>> lanes(n);
        auto addTo = [&](std::size_t lane, const std::string& from) {
            if (lane < n) lanes[lane].push_back(from);
        };
        const bool pins = routing_ == AudioRouting::PinSplit || routing_ == AudioRouting::StereoPinSplit;
        const bool split = pins || routing_ == AudioRouting::ChannelSplit;
        // syncSplitPlan's lane plan: one system, the pin splits only on a NES, and room for its lanes.
        bool splitValid = split && systems_.size() == 1 && !(pins && systems_[0].kind != SystemKind::MesenNes);
        std::vector<Stream> streams;
        std::size_t stride = 2;
        if (splitValid) {
            streams = streamsOf(systems_[0].kind, nesMode(systems_[0]));
            const bool allMono = std::none_of(streams.begin(), streams.end(), [](const Stream& s) { return s.stereo; });
            const bool pairPerMono = allMono && routing_ == AudioRouting::StereoPinSplit;
            stride = allMono && !pairPerMono ? 1 : 2;
            splitValid = stride * streams.size() <= n;
        }
        if (splitValid) {
            // ChannelSplitRouter: stream k on lane (stride * k) mod n and, for a stereo stream, the next. A
            // mono stream on a pair (StereoPinSplit) fills both lanes, as processBlock's mirror copy does.
            const System& s = systems_[0];
            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < streams.size(); ++k) {
                const std::size_t l = (stride * k) % n;
                const std::string from = sysNode(s.id) + "." + streams[k].port;
                if (streams[k].stereo) {
                    const auto [lo, ro] = d.halves(from, "stem-" + std::to_string(s.id) + "-" + std::to_string(k));
                    addTo(l, lo);
                    addTo((l + stride - 1) % n, ro);
                } else {
                    addTo(l, from);
                    if (stride == 2) addTo((l + 1) % n, from);
                }
            }
        } else {
            // MultiOutRouter, every split that fell back included: each system's L then R, in sum order.
            for (std::size_t i : order) {
                const System& s = systems_[i];
                const auto [lo, ro] = d.halves(sysNode(s.id) + ".mix", "split-" + std::to_string(s.id));
                if (routing_ == AudioRouting::OnePerInstance) {
                    addTo(i % n, lo);
                    addTo(i % n, ro);
                } else if (routing_ == AudioRouting::TwoPerInstance) {
                    const std::size_t p = (2 * i) % n;
                    addTo(p, lo);
                    addTo((p + 1) % n, ro);
                } else {
                    addTo(0, lo);
                    addTo(1, ro);
                }
            }
        }
        // Each host channel: one contribution wired straight in, several summed by rp.core.add in order.
        for (std::uint32_t c = 0; c < n; ++c) {
            buses.push_back(1);
            const auto& in = lanes[c];
            if (in.empty()) continue;
            if (in.size() == 1) {
                d.wire(in[0], outPort(c, 1));
                continue;
            }
            std::string prev;
            std::size_t at = 0;
            for (int j = 0; at < in.size(); ++j) {
                const std::string name = "lane-" + std::to_string(c) + (j ? "-" + std::to_string(j) : "");
                std::vector<std::string> ins;
                if (!prev.empty()) ins.push_back(prev + ".out");
                while (ins.size() < kAddInputs && at < in.size()) ins.push_back(in[at++]);
                d.node(name, "rp.core.add", R"({"inputs":)" + std::to_string(ins.size()) + "}");
                for (std::size_t k = 0; k < ins.size(); ++k) d.wire(ins[k], name + ".in" + std::to_string(k + 1));
                prev = name;
            }
            d.wire(prev + ".out", outPort(c, 1));
        }
    }
    std::string b = "[";
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < buses.size(); ++i) b += (i ? "," : "") + std::to_string(buses[i]);
    d.node("out", "rp.host.audio-out", R"({"buses":)" + b + "]}");
    return d.json();
}

SystemTable Translator::table() const {
    SystemTable t;
    for (const auto& s : systems_)
        t.slots.push_back({s.id, s.core, dynamic_cast<SameBoySystem*>(s.core), dynamic_cast<MesenNesSystem*>(s.core)});
    return t;
}

bool Translator::publish() { return publish_(documentJson(), std::make_shared<const SystemTable>(table())); }

}  // namespace rp::host
```

### Task 9: The engine on the graph, behind the switch

`RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE=1` (read once per process) makes `Engine` build its graph (design section 2): the
five packages, a GraphHost whose host services answer `rp.host.cores/1` and `rp.engine.kernel/1`, and the
translator, which publishes the empty project at once. In that mode:
- `processBlock` runs the plan, then today's tail (MI.OUT, `publishSystem` per system, the ppq advance);
- `QueuedInvoker` sends add / replace / remove / routing and the LinkGroup field to the translator, pushes
  every other command onto the GraphHost's ring, and a `SyncTap` behind each structural plan;
- the render calls, the audio driver, the plugin and SDL set their host shape; the engine's maximum block
  is at least 8192 (`setMaxBlockFrames` grows it).

Without the switch nothing changes: today's engine runs exactly as before, which is how every task until the
flip stays parity-green. Two pieces apply to both modes: the kernel stage moves into `runKernelStage`
(today's `runBlockWithRouter` calls it), and `AudioDriverRpcService` gets its own run flag, so the invoker's
ownership bit (which, in graph mode, hands the GraphHost back and applies what is queued) is cleared only
after the audio thread has joined.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/native/src/util/GraphEngine.hpp`
- Modify: `packages/native/src/host/engine/{Engine,EngineInvoker,SnapshotRegistry}.{hpp,cpp}`, `DspCommand.hpp`
- Modify: `packages/native/src/host/rpc/{EngineRpcService.cpp,AudioDriverRpcService.hpp,AudioDriverRpcService.cpp}`
- Modify: `packages/native/plugin/PluginDSP.cpp`, `packages/native/sdl/main.cpp`

Run: `RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE=1 node packages/retroplug/scripts/run-native-tests.mjs parity/routing-stereo 2>&1 | grep -c "graph executor"`
Expected: `0`: nothing reads the switch yet. After this task the same command prints `1`.

- [ ] **Step 2: The switch, `SyncTap` and `publishSystem`**

Create `packages/native/src/util/GraphEngine.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE=1 runs the engine on the graph executor (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md):
// the switch B1 lands behind, so today's engine stays the default until the graph one holds every parity
// case. Removed when the graph engine becomes the only one.

#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>

namespace rp {

// Read once per process, so every Engine in it (each background render has its own) runs the same way.
inline bool graphEngine() {
    static const bool on = [] {
        const char* v = std::getenv("RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE");
        return v != nullptr && std::strcmp(v, "1") == 0;
    }();
    return on;
}

}  // namespace rp
```

```diff
+// Graph mode's half of the Engine: the node packages, the GraphHost running the plan, and the translator
+// that turns structure into its documents. Destroyed first (graph_ is the Engine's last member), the
+// translator before the host it plans for.
+struct Engine::Graph {
+    rp::graph::NodeRegistry                registry;
+    rp::graph::ResourceTable               resources;
+    std::unique_ptr<rp::graph::GraphHost>  host;
+    std::unique_ptr<rp::host::Translator>  translator;
+    rp::host::HostCores                    cores{};
+    rp::host::EngineKernel                 kernel{};
+    std::uint32_t                          maxFrames = 8192;  // the matrix's largest block (B0 section 4)
+    bool                                   tracing = false;   // this block's sync trace, for the kernel stage
+    std::vector<float*>                    lanes;             // processBlockPer* lane list (control thread)
+};
+
 Engine::Engine(double sampleRate) : sampleRate_(sampleRate) {
     // Pre-reserve so adoptSystem/swapSystem never reallocate systems_ when the audio thread owns
     // the Project (production reserves the same way).
     project_.reserve(16);
+    if (!rp::graphEngine()) return;
+    static const bool announced = (std::fprintf(stderr, "[engine] RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE=1: running on the graph executor\n"), true);
+    (void)announced;
+    graph_ = std::make_unique<Graph>();
+    for (const rp_package* p : {rp::graph::corePackage(), rp::graph::hostPackage(), rp::host::sameboyPackage(),
+                                rp::host::mesenPackage(), rp::host::dspPackage()})
+        graph_->registry.addPackage(p);
+    graph_->cores = {this,
+                     [](void* ctx, std::uint64_t token) { return static_cast<Engine*>(ctx)->graph_->translator->take(token); },
+                     [](void* ctx, SystemBase* core) { static_cast<Engine*>(ctx)->registry_.release(core->id()); }};
+    graph_->kernel = {this, [](void* ctx, std::uint32_t frames) { static_cast<Engine*>(ctx)->runKernelStage(frames); }};
+    rp::graph::HostConfig config;
+    config.sampleRate = sampleRate;
+    config.maxFrames = graph_->maxFrames;
+    config.interfaces = &Engine::graphInterface;
+    config.interfacesCtx = this;
+    graph_->host = std::make_unique<rp::graph::GraphHost>(graph_->registry, graph_->resources, config);
+    graph_->host->setHostCommandHandler(&Engine::onHostCommand, this);
+    graph_->translator = std::make_unique<rp::host::Translator>(
+        [this](const std::string& json, std::shared_ptr<const rp::host::SystemTable> table) {
+            auto doc = rp::graph::parseGraphDocument(json);
+            if (!doc) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "[engine] the translator built a document that does not parse: %s\n", doc.error().what().c_str());
+                return false;
+            }
+            const auto result = graph_->host->setGraph(*doc, std::move(table));
+            for (const auto& e : result.errors)
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "[engine] a graph was refused (%s) at %s.%s: %s\n", e.code.c_str(), e.nodeId.c_str(),
+                             e.port.c_str(), e.message.c_str());
+            return result.ok;
+        },
+        [this](SystemBase* core) { registry_.release(core->id()); });
+}
+
+Engine::~Engine() = default;
+
+const void* Engine::graphInterface(void* ctx, const char* id) {
+    auto* self = static_cast<Engine*>(ctx);
+    if (std::strcmp(id, RP_HOST_CORES_INTERFACE) == 0) return &self->graph_->cores;
+    if (std::strcmp(id, RP_ENGINE_KERNEL_INTERFACE) == 0) return &self->graph_->kernel;
+    return nullptr;
+}
+
+rp::host::Translator& Engine::translator() { return *graph_->translator; }
+
+bool Engine::pushCommand(const DspCommand& c) { return graph_->host->pushHostCommand(&c, sizeof c); }
+
+void Engine::drainCommands() { graph_->host->drain(); }
+
+void Engine::setAudioThreadOwns(bool owns) { graph_->host->setAudioThreadOwns(owns); }
+
+std::size_t Engine::reclaim() { return graph_->host->reclaim(); }
+
+void Engine::discardPending() { graph_->host->discardPending(); }
+
+void Engine::setMaxBlockFrames(std::uint32_t frames) {
+    if (!graph_ || frames <= graph_->maxFrames) return;
+    std::string why;
+    if (graph_->host->reactivate(sampleRate_, frames, &why)) graph_->maxFrames = frames;
+    else std::fprintf(stderr, "[Engine] could not grow the block size to %u: %s\n", static_cast<unsigned>(frames), why.c_str());
+}
+
+void Engine::setSampleRate(double sr) {
+    sampleRate_ = sr;
+    if (!graph_) {
+        project_.onSampleRateChanged(sr);
+        return;
+    }
+    // Project::onSampleRateChanged's calls, in slot order; then the plan's buffers at the new rate.
+    for (SystemBase* core : graph_->translator->cores()) core->onSampleRateChanged(sr);
+    std::string why;
+    if (!graph_->host->reactivate(sr, graph_->maxFrames, &why))
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "[Engine] could not re-activate the graph at %g Hz: %s\n", sr, why.c_str());
+}
+
+const rp::host::SystemTable& Engine::currentTable() const {
+    static const rp::host::SystemTable kEmpty;
+    const auto* t = static_cast<const rp::host::SystemTable*>(graph_->host->hostData());
+    return t ? *t : kEmpty;
+}
+
+void Engine::onHostCommand(void* ctx, const rp::graph::HostCommand& cmd, const void*, bool discard) {
+    static_assert(sizeof(DspCommand) <= rp::graph::kHostCommandBytes, "a DspCommand rides the ring as a HostCommand");
+    static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<DspCommand>, "a DspCommand is copied as bytes");
+    DspCommand c;
+    std::memcpy(&c, cmd.bytes, sizeof c);
+    if (!discard) {
+        static_cast<Engine*>(ctx)->applyCommand(c);
+        return;
+    }
+    switch (c.kind) {  // teardown: free what it owns, as QueuedInvoker::freePending does
+        case DspCommand::Kind::SetSystems:      delete c.setSystems.json; break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::LoadKernel:      delete c.loadKernel.bytecode; break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::WriteRam:        delete c.writeRam.bytes; break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::StageMidi:       delete c.stageMidi.ext; break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::StageSystemMidi: delete c.stageSystemMidi.ext; break;
+        default: break;
+    }
+}
+
+// QueuedInvoker::drainInto's switch for every kind graph mode still queues, plus SyncTap. The structural
+// kinds never arrive here: the translator turns them into plans.
+void Engine::applyCommand(DspCommand& cmd) {
+    switch (cmd.kind) {
+        case DspCommand::Kind::SetSystems:
+            setSystems(std::vector<std::uint8_t>(cmd.setSystems.json->begin(), cmd.setSystems.json->end()));
+            delete cmd.setSystems.json;
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::LoadKernel:
+            loadKernel(*cmd.loadKernel.bytecode);
+            delete cmd.loadKernel.bytecode;
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::StageMidi:
+            if (cmd.stageMidi.ext) {
+                stageMidi(std::move(*cmd.stageMidi.ext));
+                delete cmd.stageMidi.ext;
+            } else {
+                stageMidi(std::vector<std::uint8_t>(cmd.stageMidi.data, cmd.stageMidi.data + cmd.stageMidi.len));
+            }
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::StageControllerMidi:
+            stageControllerMidi(std::vector<std::uint8_t>(cmd.stageMidi.data, cmd.stageMidi.data + cmd.stageMidi.len));
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::StageSystemMidi:
+            if (cmd.stageSystemMidi.ext) {
+                stageSystemMidi(cmd.stageSystemMidi.id, cmd.stageSystemMidi.ext->data(), cmd.stageSystemMidi.ext->size());
+                delete cmd.stageSystemMidi.ext;
+            } else {
+                stageSystemMidi(cmd.stageSystemMidi.id, cmd.stageSystemMidi.data, cmd.stageSystemMidi.len);
+            }
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::SetBpm:          setBpm(cmd.setBpm.value); break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::SetTransport:    setTransport(cmd.setTransport.value); break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::SetPpq:          setPpq(cmd.setPpq.value); break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::SetConfigField:
+            applyConfigField(cmd.setConfigField.id, cmd.setConfigField.field, cmd.setConfigField.value);
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::PressButton:
+            pressButton(cmd.pressButton.id, cmd.pressButton.button, cmd.pressButton.down);
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::WriteRam:
+            writeRam(cmd.writeRam.id, cmd.writeRam.offset, *cmd.writeRam.bytes);
+            delete cmd.writeRam.bytes;
+            break;
+        case DspCommand::Kind::SyncTap:
+            syncTap(static_cast<AudioRouting>(cmd.syncTap.routing));
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+    }
+}
+
+// syncSplitPlan's arming half, against the plan just adopted (design section 3.2): restore whatever was
+// armed, then arm the mode the routing wants. A core that has gone (removed, or replaced under a new id)
+// is not found, and its record is dropped, as removeSystem and replaceSystem drop it today.
+void Engine::syncTap(AudioRouting routing) {
+    const rp::host::SystemTable& table = currentTable();
+    if (armedTap_.active) {
+        if (const auto* e = table.find(armedTap_.id); e && e->nes) e->nes->setChannelExportMode(armedTap_.prevMode);
+        armedTap_ = {};
+    }
+    const bool pins  = routing == AudioRouting::PinSplit || routing == AudioRouting::StereoPinSplit;
+    const bool split = pins || routing == AudioRouting::ChannelSplit;
+    if (!split || table.slots.size() != 1) return;
+    MesenNesSystem* nes = table.slots.front().nes;
+    if (!nes) return;
+    const std::uint32_t want = pins ? 1u : 3u;
+    if (nes->channelExportMode() != want) {
+        armedTap_ = { true, nes->id(), nes->channelExportMode() };
+        nes->setChannelExportMode(want);
+    }
 }

+// Graph mode: the shape (rp::host::HostShape) decides who goes where; the RPC set it before rendering, so
+// these only lay the pairs out as one channel list.
+void Engine::processBlockGraph(std::uint32_t frames, float* const* outputs, std::size_t numOutputs) {
+    blockTracing_ = syncTrace_.beginBlock(frames, sampleRate_, bpm_, ppq_, transport_);
+    const rp_transport transport{sampleRate_, bpm_, ppq_, transport_ ? RP_TRANSPORT_PLAYING : 0u};
+    // The kernel stage, every system, the mixing and the host outputs (the host drained before this).
+    graph_->host->run(frames, transport, outputs, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(numOutputs));
+    const rp::host::SystemTable& table = currentTable();
+    // MI.OUT: as runBlockWithRouter gathers it, in slot order with each byte's frame dropped.
+    if (dspActive_)
+        for (const auto& e : table.slots) {
+            if (!e.sameboy || e.sameboy->serialOutLog_.empty()) continue;
+            for (const auto& entry : e.sameboy->serialOutLog_) pendingSerialOut_.push_back({ e.id, entry.second });
+        }
+    for (const auto& e : table.slots) registry_.publishSystem(*e.core, frames, sampleRate_);
+    if (blockTracing_) syncTrace_.endBlock();
+    if (transport_)
+        ppq_ += (bpm_ / 60.0) * (static_cast<double>(frames) / sampleRate_);
+}
```

```diff
 // --- producer half (control thread): pack + push, then flush inline unless the audio thread drains ---
 
+bool QueuedInvoker::graph() const { return engine_ && engine_->graphMode(); }
+
+bool QueuedInvoker::push(const DspCommand& c) {
+    return graph() ? engine_->pushCommand(c) : commands_.tryPush(c);
+}
+
+// Graph mode: the arming syncSplitPlan does at every structural change, right behind its plan.
+void QueuedInvoker::syncTap() {
+    DspCommand c;
+    c.kind = DspCommand::Kind::SyncTap;
+    c.syncTap = { static_cast<std::uint8_t>(engine_->translator().routing()) };
+    push(c);
+}
+
+void QueuedInvoker::setHostShape(rp::host::HostShape shape) {
+    if (graph()) engine_->translator().setShape(shape);
+}
+

+    if (graph()) {
+        if (engine_->translator().add(std::move(sys))) syncTap();
+        return;

     c.kind = DspCommand::Kind::AddSystem;

     else if (registry_) registry_->release(sys->id());     // full ring: unique_ptr deletes the build → free its slot
     maybeFlush();
 }

-    if (commands_.tryPush(c)) sys.release();
+    if (push(c)) sys.release();
     else if (registry_) registry_->release(sys->id());     // full ring: unique_ptr deletes the build → free its slot
     maybeFlush();
 }
```

Expected: `# 24 native test file(s) passed` (today's engine, the switch off).

- [ ] **Step 7: Run parity on the graph**

Run: `RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE=1 RP_FAIL_ON_SKIP=1 RETROPLUG_RESOURCES_DIR=/workspaces/resources node packages/retroplug/scripts/run-native-tests.mjs parity`
Expected: each file prints `[engine] RETROPLUG_GRAPH_ENGINE=1: running on the graph executor`; then
`# 24 native test file(s) passed`, every group `0 skipped, 0 failed`. This is the progress meter: at the
prototype every case passed on the first full run. A case that fails names its first differing segment and
lane; `RP_PARITY_DUMP=<dir>` writes both runs' lanes as WAVs to compare (B0 section 5.4).

### Task 10: Accept before the flip

Nothing to write: the acceptance B0 section 4 sets for a B step, in both switch states, before the old engine
is deleted. Every command must exit 0; a failure is fixed before Task 11.

Expected: `# 148 native test file(s) passed` both times.

Expected: each ends `PASS:`; `build/reaper-cfg-editor-reopen/logs/reaper.log` contains `running on the graph
executor`. The click-loading checks retry on their own and can still end `SKIP: could not click-load mGB`
(exit 2, inconclusive): run that one again.

Expected: `==> host passed under thread sanitizer`, both slugs `1 passed, 0 skipped`.

### Task 11: The flip

The graph engine becomes the only engine. Deleted: the routers and `BlockRunner`, `OfflineRender` (dead
code), `LinkGroup`, `Project`, `AudioRouting.hpp` (its enum moves into `Translator.hpp` as
`rp::host::AudioRouting`), `DspEvent.hpp` and the invoker's two rings, `syncSplitPlan` and its lane plan,
the structural `DspCommand` kinds, `processBlockPerSystem` / `processBlockPerChannel` (the render calls hand
`processBlock` their pairs as one channel list), `SystemBase::isLinked`, `SnapshotRegistry::publishAll`,
and the switch. `QueuedInvoker` is constructed from the engine alone. Three Catch2 files whose subject is
gone are deleted (the routers, `runUnit` over a fake system, the Engine's split gating over fake systems:
the translator and system-node tests and the parity split cases hold that behaviour now), and four are
ported off the deleted API.

**Files:**
- Delete: `packages/native/src/system/BlockRunner.{hpp,cpp}`, `OfflineRender.{hpp,cpp}`, `AudioRouting.hpp`,
  `packages/native/src/system/sameboy/LinkGroup.{hpp,cpp}`, `packages/native/src/project/Project.{hpp,cpp}`,
  `packages/native/src/host/engine/DspEvent.hpp`, `packages/native/src/util/GraphEngine.hpp`,
  `packages/native/test/audio/{ChannelSplit,ChannelStreams,EngineChannelSplit}.test.cpp`
- Modify: the engine, invoker, registry, RPC, translator, system and test files below

Create `packages/native/src/host/engine/EngineInvoker.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "system/SystemTypes.hpp"  // SystemId

#include "host/engine/DspCommand.hpp"

class Engine;
class SystemBase;
namespace rp::host { struct HostShape; }

// The ONE mutation path to the Engine. Structure (add / replace / remove, the routing mode, a Game Boy's
// link group, the host's output shape) goes to the Engine's translator, which plans it as a graph
// document; every other edit is packed into a POD DspCommand and pushed onto the GraphHost's one ring
// (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 5), so all of them keep their push order. Who DRAINS the
// ring is the only mode, held in `audioThreadOwns_`:
//   - while the audio thread owns the Engine (running), it drains each block; the producer just pushes.
//   - otherwise every push applies inline (the GraphHost flushes as it is pushed onto), so the ring is
//     empty at the moment the audio thread takes ownership, and the handoff is clean.
// Reads never go through here (they read the snapshot registry). A removed or displaced core leaves with
// the plan that last ran it, and is freed on the control thread when that plan is reclaimed.
class QueuedInvoker {
public:
    explicit QueuedInvoker(Engine& engine) : engine_(&engine) {}

    // Drain ownership. Set true BEFORE spawning/activating the audio thread (pushes become push-only);
    // set false AFTER joining it (pushes apply inline again, and whatever is queued applies now). Written
    // on the control thread only; read by the control thread and the audio loop (its run condition).
    void setAudioThreadOwns(bool owns);
    bool audioThreadOwns() const { return audioThreadOwns_.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }

    // producer (control thread)
    void adoptSystem(std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> sys);
    void replaceSystem(SystemId id, std::unique_ptr<SystemBase> sys);
    void removeSystem(SystemId id);
    void loadKernel(std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytecode);
    void setSystems(std::string json);
    // Any length: a channel message rides the command inline, a longer run (SysEx) as an owning payload.
    void stageMidi(std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes);
    // Control-surface traffic (a pad press), kept out of the musical stream. Inline-only (4 bytes),
    // which a Note/CC press fits inside; a surface's sysex never comes this way. False, with nothing
    // staged, for an empty message or one longer than 4 bytes.
    bool stageControllerMidi(std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes);
    // MIDI for ONE system, bypassing the routing kernel entirely (see DspCommand::stageSystemMidi).
    void stageSystemMidi(SystemId id, std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes);
    void setBpm(double bpm);
    void setTransport(bool playing);
    void setPpq(double ppq);
    void setAudioRouting(std::uint8_t mode);
    void applyConfigField(SystemId id, std::uint8_t field, double value);
    void pressButton(SystemId id, std::uint8_t button, bool down);
    void writeRam(SystemId id, std::uint32_t offset, std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes);
    // The output shape the next blocks render into. The render calls set it before they render.
    void setHostShape(rp::host::HostShape shape);

    // consumer: apply every queued command (the block thread, or the control thread while quiescent).
    void drainInto(Engine& engine);
    // control thread: free the plans the audio thread handed back, and the cores only they held. Returns
    // how many plans it freed.
    std::uint32_t reclaimReleased();
    // after the audio thread is joined: DISCARD what is still queued (its payloads freed, never applied).
    // Teardown only: drainInto APPLIES a pending command; this drops it.
    void freePending();

private:
    bool push(const DspCommand& c);
    // The NES tap arming every structural change does, pushed right behind its plan.
    void syncTap();

    Engine*           engine_ = nullptr;
    std::atomic<bool> audioThreadOwns_{false};
};
```

`packages/native/src/host/graph/Translator.hpp`:

```diff
diff --git a/packages/native/src/host/graph/Translator.hpp b/packages/native/src/host/graph/Translator.hpp
index 5c6baee1..eaeabc11 100644
--- a/packages/native/src/host/graph/Translator.hpp
+++ b/packages/native/src/host/graph/Translator.hpp
@@ -8,13 +8,48 @@
 #include <vector>
 
 #include "host/graph/SystemTable.hpp"
-#include "system/AudioRouting.hpp"
 #include "system/SystemTypes.hpp"
 
 class SystemBase;
 
 namespace rp::host {
 
+// How per-system audio fans out across the 8 plugin output channels. The
+// plugin always declares 8 outputs (DISTRHO_PLUGIN_NUM_OUTPUTS); modes
+// pick which of those get written, the rest are zero.
+//   Stereo          all systems sum into outs 0/1; outs 2..7 silent.
+//   TwoPerInstance  system i writes to outs (2i % 8)/(2i % 8 + 1).
+//   OnePerInstance  system i writes a mono mix to out (i % 8).
+//   ChannelSplit    ONE system fans its per-channel streams across the outputs
+//                   (a Game Boy's 4 STEREO channels -> outs 0/1,2/3,4/5,6/7; a
+//                   NES's 5 MONO core channels -> outs 0..4).
+//   PinSplit        ONE NES fans its three 2A03 output-pin streams (Pulse | TND
+//                   | lumped Expansion) across outs 0..2, mono. NES-only: any
+//                   other single system falls back to Stereo.
+//   StereoPinSplit  the same three pins, but each on its OWN stereo PAIR (outs
+//                   0/1, 2/3, 4/5) with the mono stem mirrored into both lanes.
+//                   Six lanes instead of three, and the reason to spend them is
+//                   the DAW: every pin arrives as a normal centred stereo track,
+//                   so hosts that can only bus/FX whole stereo pairs can treat
+//                   each pin separately. Under PinSplit the pins share pairs
+//                   (Pulse on L of track 1, TND on its R), which those hosts
+//                   cannot pull apart. NES-only, like PinSplit.
+//
+// All three split modes are single-system only; any other project, and a single
+// system the host is too narrow for, falls back to Stereo.
+//
+// Modes 0..2 fan MANY systems across the fixed pairs; modes 3..5 split ONE system's
+// channels (a pair per stream, or a lane per mono stream). The routing value is owned
+// by TS and pushed through the SetAudioRouting RPC; the translator wires it.
+enum class AudioRouting : std::uint8_t {
+    Stereo         = 0,
+    TwoPerInstance = 1,
+    OnePerInstance = 2,
+    ChannelSplit   = 3,
+    PinSplit       = 4,
+    StereoPinSplit = 5,
+};
+
 // The output shape a document is built for (docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md section 4.4): the host's
 // own outputs routed by the project's mode (Multi), each system on its own pair (PerSystem), or one
 // system's streams on a pair each (PerChannel).
@@ -25,8 +60,8 @@ struct HostShape {
     bool operator==(const HostShape&) const = default;
 };
 
-// Turns today's structural calls into graph documents (section 4). Control thread only. It mirrors
-// Project: the ordered system list, each Game Boy's link group, the routing mode and the host shape, and

+// ordered system list, each Game Boy's link group, the routing mode and the host shape, and
 // after every change it builds the document and its system table and hands both to `publish` (the
 // engine's GraphHost::setGraph), which answers whether the document planned.
 //
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Port the tests off the deleted API**

`SameBoyStems` and `NesStems` drove `finishBlock` through `runUnit` and a router: they now drive the triad
directly into the same lanes. `EngineSampleRate` adopted fake systems, which no system node can take: it
now drives a real Game Boy through the invoker. `NesSplitRouting` edits through the invoker, which is how
every host edits.

Create `packages/native/test/audio/EngineSampleRate.test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Guards that a host sample-rate change reaches ALREADY-LIVE cores. DPF's Plugin::sampleRateChanged (fired
// only while deactivated) calls Engine::setSampleRate, which must re-rate every adopted system in place —
// SameBoy's GB_set_sample_rate, Mesen's resampler — not just bake the new rate into cores built afterwards.
// Regression guard for the wiring that was missing: Engine::setSampleRate only updated its own scalar, so a
// loaded project kept rendering at its construct-time rate after the host switched (wrong pitch/tempo).
// Drives a real Game Boy through the engine and reads the rate its core holds.
//
// Run via `pnpm test:plugin`.

#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>

#include "host/engine/Engine.hpp"
#include "host/engine/EngineInvoker.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoyConfig.hpp"
#include "system/sameboy/SameBoySystem.hpp"

#ifndef RP_MGB_ROM_PATH
#error "RP_MGB_ROM_PATH must be defined (path to resources/roms/mGB.gb)"
#endif

namespace {

std::vector<std::uint8_t> readRom() {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes;
    if (std::FILE* f = std::fopen(RP_MGB_ROM_PATH, "rb")) {
        std::fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
        bytes.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(std::ftell(f)));
        std::fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
        if (std::fread(bytes.data(), 1, bytes.size(), f) != bytes.size()) bytes.clear();
        std::fclose(f);
    }
    return bytes;
}

} // namespace

TEST_CASE("Engine::setSampleRate re-rates already-live systems", "[audio][samplerate][engine]") {
    Engine        eng(44100.0);
    QueuedInvoker inv(eng);

    auto sys = std::make_unique<SameBoySystem>(1, SameBoyConfig{}, readRom());
    sys->onActivate(eng.sampleRate());  // built at the engine's current rate, as the factory does
    SameBoySystem* raw = sys.get();
    inv.adoptSystem(std::move(sys));
    REQUIRE(eng.findSystem(1) == raw);
    REQUIRE(raw->sampleRate_ == 44100.0);

    // A host sample-rate change (via DPF sampleRateChanged) must reach the live core.
    eng.setSampleRate(48000.0);
    CHECK(raw->sampleRate_ == 48000.0);
    CHECK(eng.sampleRate() == 48000.0);

    // A second change also propagates (not a one-shot), and the core still renders a block.
    eng.setSampleRate(96000.0);
    CHECK(raw->sampleRate_ == 96000.0);
    std::vector<float> l(512), r(512);
    eng.processBlock(512, l.data(), r.data());
}

TEST_CASE("Engine::setSampleRate on an empty project is a harmless no-op", "[audio][samplerate][engine]") {
    // The CLI + plugin set the rate before any system exists; that must not fault and must stick for the
    // cores built next.
    Engine eng(44100.0);
    eng.setSampleRate(48000.0);
    CHECK(eng.sampleRate() == 48000.0);
}
```

### Task 12: Docs

The architecture docs described `Project`, `BlockRunner`, the routers, `LinkGroup`, the release ring of
cores and `syncSplitPlan`; they now describe the graph engine: the per-block order, the translator and its
routing-as-wiring table, the host shapes, the core's life from build to reclaim (parked, taken by its node,
freed with the plan that last held it), the one ring carrying plans and commands in push order, the
`SyncTap`, and the ten `test:plugin` binaries. The B1 design's status line and §8 of the main design say B1
is built. The prototype's docs pass is a commit of its own, so this task takes it whole.

**Files:**
- Modify: `AGENTS.md`, `docs/modular-graph-design.md`, `docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md`, `spec/00-overview.md`, `spec/01-architecture.md`, `spec/02-native-host.md`, `spec/06-build-test.md`, `spec/10-multichannel-audio-out.md`, `spec/13-subsystems.md`, `spec/README.md`

Expected: no output. (Under `docs/`, the phase plans and designs and `sms-support.md` are dated records and
keep their references.)
Every relative link in the changed files must resolve:
`for f in AGENTS.md docs/modular-graph-design.md docs/modular-graph-phase-b1-design.md spec/00-overview.md spec/01-architecture.md spec/02-native-host.md spec/06-build-test.md spec/10-multichannel-audio-out.md spec/13-subsystems.md spec/README.md; do grep -o "](\.\./[^)#]*\|](\./[^)#]*\|]([a-zA-Z0-9_-]*\.md" "$f" | sed 's/^](//' | while read l; do [ -e "$(dirname "$f")/$l" ] || echo "$f: $l"; done; done`
